<!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "\"\"\"\nVersion Module for KosDWM\n=========================\n\nProvides version information from AUTOVERSION.py\n\"\"\"\n\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\ndef get_version():\n    \"\"\"Get current KosDWM version.\"\"\"\n    # Look for AUTOVERSION.py in parent directories\n    current = Path(__file__).parent\n    for parent in [current.parent] + list(current.parents):\n        version_file = parent / \"AUTOVERSION.py\"\n        if version_file.exists():\n            # Execute the file to get VERSION\n            namespace = {}\n            exec(open(version_file).read(), namespace)\n            return namespace.get(\"VERSION\", \"unknown\")\n    return \"unknown\"\n\n\ndef get_version_info():\n    \"\"\"Get detailed version info.\"\"\"\n    version = get_version()\n    return {\n        \"version\": version,\n        \"name\": \"KosDWM\",\n        \"full\": f\"KosDWM {version}\"\n    }\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    print(get_version())\n", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 213, "timestamp": 1784915091.3272984, "date": "2026-07-24", "name": "ReadFile"} -->

<!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nKosDWM - PyQt5 Version\nA simple window manager built with PyQt5\n\"\"\"\n\nimport sys\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Add src to path\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent / \"src\"))\n\nfrom PyQt5.QtWidgets import QApplication, QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout\nfrom PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QTimer\nfrom PyQt5.QtGui import QFont\n\nfrom src.panel_pyqt5 import Panel\n\n\nclass KosDWM(QWidget):\n    \"\"\"\n    Main KosDWM window manager - Top panel only\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        super().__init__()\n        \n        # Make it a top-level window that stays on top\n        self.setWindowFlags(\n            Qt.FramelessWindowHint | \n            Qt.WindowStaysOnTopHint |\n            Qt.Tool\n        )\n        \n        # Set fixed height for panel (30px)\n        self.setFixedHeight(30)\n        \n        # Position at top of screen, full width\n        screen = QApplication.primaryScreen().geometry()\n        self.setGeometry(0, 0, screen.width(), 30)\n        \n        # Style\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #333333;\")\n        \n        # Create panel\n        self.create_panel()\n        \n        print(\"KosDWM PyQt5 panel initialized\")\n    \n    def create_panel(self):\n        \"\"\"Create the top panel\"\"\"\n        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)\n        layout.setSpacing(0)\n        \n        self.panel = Panel(self)\n        layout.addWidget(self.panel)\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main entry point\"\"\"\n    app = QApplication(sys.argv)\n    \n    # Set application font\n    font = QFont(\"Arial\", 10)\n    app.setFont(font)\n    \n    # Create main window (panel)\n    window = KosDWM()\n    window.show()\n    \n    print(\"KosDWM PyQt5 panel started!\")\n    print(f\"Position: {window.geometry()}\")\n    \n    return app.exec_()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 213, "timestamp": 1784915092.663046, "date": "2026-07-24", "name": "ReadFile"} -->

<!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nPanel widget for KosDWM PyQt5 version\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom PyQt5.QtWidgets import (\n    QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton, \n    QComboBox, QFrame, QMenu, QAction, QButtonGroup\n)\nfrom PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QTimer\nfrom PyQt5.QtGui import QFont\n\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\n\nclass Panel(QFrame):\n    \"\"\"\n    Top panel with gadgets, menus, clock, and desktop switcher\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, parent=None):\n        super().__init__(parent)\n        # Height is controlled by parent window (30px)\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QFrame {\n                background-color: #333333;\n                border: none;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 8px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #555555;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: white;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        # Setup desktop manager\n        from src.desktop_manager_pyqt5 import DesktopManager\n        self.desktop_manager = DesktopManager(self)\n        self.desktop_manager.desktop_changed.connect(self.on_desktop_changed)\n        \n        # Setup menu manager\n        from src.menus_pyqt5 import MenuManager\n        self.menu_manager = MenuManager(self)\n        \n        # Setup window manager\n        from src.window_manager_pyqt5 import WindowManager\n        self.window_manager = WindowManager()\n        \n        self.setup_ui()\n        \n        # Timer for clock updates\n        self.timer = QTimer()\n        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_time)\n        self.timer.start(1000)\n        \n        # Timer for window list updates\n        self.window_timer = QTimer()\n        self.window_timer.timeout.connect(self.update_window_list)\n        self.window_timer.start(2000)  # Update every 2 seconds\n    \n    def setup_ui(self):\n        \"\"\"Setup the panel UI\"\"\"\n        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(5, 2, 5, 2)\n        layout.setSpacing(5)\n        \n        # Left side: Menus button with dynamic menu\n        self.menus_button = QPushButton(\"\u2630\")\n        self.menus_button.setFixedWidth(30)\n        self.menus_button.setMenu(self.build_menus_menu())\n        layout.addWidget(self.menus_button)\n        \n        # Window switcher dropdown\n        self.window_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.window_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\")\n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(self.window_manager.is_available())\n        self.window_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_window_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.window_combo)\n        \n        # Desktop buttons\n        self.desktop_group = QButtonGroup(self)\n        self.desktop_buttons = []\n        for i in range(4):\n            btn = QPushButton(str(i + 1))\n            btn.setFixedWidth(25)\n            btn.setFixedHeight(22)\n            btn.setCheckable(True)\n            btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked, d=i: self.switch_desktop(d))\n            self.desktop_group.addButton(btn)\n            self.desktop_buttons.append(btn)\n            layout.addWidget(btn)\n        \n        # Middle: Gadgets frame\n        self.gadgets_frame = QFrame()\n        self.gadgets_frame.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: transparent;\")\n        self.gadgets_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.gadgets_frame)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setSpacing(5)\n        layout.addWidget(self.gadgets_frame)\n        \n        layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # Clock\n        self.time_label = QLabel(\"00:00\")\n        self.time_label.setFont(QFont(\"Arial\", 10, QFont.Bold))\n        layout.addWidget(self.time_label)\n        \n        # Config dropdown\n        self.config_combo = QComboBox()\n        # Config dropdown\n        self.config_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.config_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\u2699 Config\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Manage Gadgets\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Panel Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Menu Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Date/Time Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"About KosDWM\")\n        self.config_combo.setFixedWidth(120)\n        self.config_combo.setFixedHeight(22)\n        self.config_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_config_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.config_combo)\n        \n        # Load gadgets\n        self.load_gadgets()\n        \n        # Set current desktop\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        \n        # Initial window list population\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_window_list(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the window switcher dropdown with current windows\"\"\"\n        if not self.window_manager.is_available():\n            self.window_combo.setEnabled(False)\n            return\n        \n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(True)\n        \n        # Get current selection before clearing\n        current_id = None\n        current_index = self.window_combo.currentIndex()\n        if current_index > 0:  # 0 is placeholder\n            current_data = self.window_combo.itemData(current_index)\n            if current_data:\n                current_id = current_data.get('id')\n        \n        # Block signals while updating\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.window_combo.clear()\n        \n        # Add placeholder with icon\n        self.window_combo.addItem(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\", None)\n        \n        # Get windows and add to dropdown\n        windows = self.window_manager.get_windows()\n        selected_index = 0\n        \n        for i, window in enumerate(windows):\n            # Truncate long titles\n            title = window['title']\n            display_title = title[:32] + \"...\" if len(title) > 35 else title\n            \n            # Add desktop number prefix if not on current desktop\n            display_text = display_title\n            desktop_prefix = \"\"\n            if window['desktop'] != -1:  # -1 means sticky/all desktops\n                current_desktop = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n                if window['desktop'] != current_desktop:\n                    desktop_prefix = f\"[{window['desktop']+1}] \"\n                    display_text = f\"{desktop_prefix}{display_title}\"\n            \n            index = self.window_combo.count()\n            self.window_combo.addItem(display_text, window)\n            \n            # Set tooltip with full info\n            tooltip = f\"{desktop_prefix}{window['name']}\\nDesktop: {window['desktop']+1 if window['desktop'] >= 0 else 'All'}\"\n            self.window_combo.setItemData(index, tooltip, Qt.ToolTipRole)\n            \n            # Restore selection if this window was selected\n            if current_id and window['id'] == current_id:\n                selected_index = index\n        \n        # Restore selection\n        self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(selected_index)\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(False)\n    \n    def on_window_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle window selection from dropdown\"\"\"\n        if index <= 0:  # Placeholder selected\n            return\n        \n        window = self.window_combo.itemData(index)\n        if window and 'id' in window:\n            # Visual feedback - briefly highlight the combo\n            self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QComboBox {\n                    background-color: #666666;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 5px;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                    min-width: 120px;\n                    max-width: 200px;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            \n            # Activate the window\n            success = self.window_manager.activate_window(window['id'])\n            \n            # Reset to placeholder after activation\n            self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            \n            # Reset style after a short delay\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer\n            QTimer.singleShot(200, self._reset_window_combo_style)\n    \n    def _reset_window_combo_style(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset window combo to normal style\"\"\"\n        self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    def load_gadgets(self):\n        \"\"\"Load gadgets into the panel\"\"\"\n        from src.gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetManager\n        \n        # Clear existing gadgets\n        while self.gadgets_layout.count():\n            item = self.gadgets_layout.takeAt(0)\n            if item.widget():\n                item.widget().deleteLater()\n        \n        # Create gadget manager\n        self.gadget_manager = GadgetManager()\n        \n        # Store gadget->button mapping for refresh\n        self._gadget_buttons = {}\n        \n        # Add enabled gadgets\n        for gadget in self.gadget_manager.get_enabled_gadgets():\n            btn = QPushButton(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setFixedSize(40, 22)\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n            # Store gadget reference on button to avoid lambda capture issues\n            btn._gadget = gadget\n            btn.clicked.connect(self._on_gadget_clicked)\n            self.gadgets_layout.addWidget(btn)\n            # Store mapping\n            self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()] = btn\n            # Set panel reference on gadget for refresh notifications\n            gadget.set_panel(self)\n    \n    def refresh_gadget_icon(self, gadget):\n        \"\"\"Refresh icon for a specific gadget\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, '_gadget_buttons') and gadget.get_name() in self._gadget_buttons:\n            btn = self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()]\n            btn.setText(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n    \n    def _on_gadget_clicked(self):\n        \"\"\"Handle gadget button click\"\"\"\n        btn = self.sender()\n        if btn and hasattr(btn, '_gadget'):\n            try:\n                btn._gadget.on_click()\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in gadget click: {e}\")\n                import traceback\n                traceback.print_exc()\n    \n    def update_time(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the clock\"\"\"\n        current = datetime.now().strftime(\"%H:%M\")\n        self.time_label.setText(current)\n    \n    def switch_desktop(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Switch to specified desktop\"\"\"\n        self.desktop_manager.switch_to_desktop(desktop_id)\n        # Update window list to show new desktop's windows\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def on_desktop_changed(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Handle desktop change\"\"\"\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_desktop_buttons(self):\n        \"\"\"Update desktop button states\"\"\"\n        current = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n        for i, btn in enumerate(self.desktop_buttons):\n            btn.setChecked(i == current)\n            if i == current:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #666666; font-weight: bold;\")\n            else:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #4a4a4a;\")\n    \n    def build_menus_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Build the dynamic menus\"\"\"\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        \n        # Light theme for menu\n        menu.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QMenu {\n                background-color: #f5f5f5;\n                color: #333333;\n                border: 1px solid #cccccc;\n                padding: 5px;\n            }\n            QMenu::item {\n                padding: 8px 25px;\n                border-radius: 3px;\n                color: #333333;\n            }\n            QMenu::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: white;\n            }\n            QMenu::separator {\n                height: 1px;\n                background-color: #cccccc;\n                margin: 5px 0px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        # Load dynamic menus from directory structure\n        for menu_data in self.menu_manager.load_menus():\n            if menu_data.get(\"type\") == \"branch\":\n                # Create top-level submenu\n                submenu = menu.addMenu(menu_data[\"name\"])\n                self.menu_manager._populate_menu(submenu, menu_data.get(\"items\", []))\n        \n        menu.addSeparator()\n        menu.addAction(\"Exit\", self.window().close)\n        \n        return menu\n    \n    def on_config_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle configuration dropdown selection\"\"\"\n        if index < 0:\n            return\n        \n        # Block signals temporarily to prevent re-triggering when resetting\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.config_combo.setCurrentIndex(-1)\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(False)\n        \n        # Open appropriate dialog\n        if index == 0:\n            self.open_gadget_config()\n        elif index == 1:\n            self.open_panel_config()\n        elif index == 2:\n            self.open_menu_config()\n        elif index == 3:\n            self.open_datetime_config()\n        elif index == 4:\n            self.open_about_dialog()\n    \n    def open_gadget_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open gadget configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.gadget_config_pyqt5 import GadgetConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = GadgetConfigDialog(self.gadget_manager, self)\n        if dialog.exec_():\n            self.load_gadgets()\n    \n    def open_panel_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open panel configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.panel_config_pyqt5 import PanelConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = PanelConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_menu_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open menu configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.menu_config_pyqt5 import MenuConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = MenuConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_datetime_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open date/time configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.datetime_config_pyqt5 import DateTimeConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = DateTimeConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nPanel widget for KosDWM PyQt5 version\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom PyQt5.QtWidgets import (\n    QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton, \n    QComboBox, QFrame, QMenu, QAction, QButtonGroup\n)\nfrom PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QTimer\nfrom PyQt5.QtGui import QFont\n\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\n\nclass Panel(QFrame):\n    \"\"\"\n    Top panel with gadgets, menus, clock, and desktop switcher\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, parent=None):\n        super().__init__(parent)\n        # Height is controlled by parent window (30px)\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QFrame {\n                background-color: #333333;\n                border: none;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 8px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #555555;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: white;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        # Setup desktop manager\n        from src.desktop_manager_pyqt5 import DesktopManager\n        self.desktop_manager = DesktopManager(self)\n        self.desktop_manager.desktop_changed.connect(self.on_desktop_changed)\n        \n        # Setup menu manager\n        from src.menus_pyqt5 import MenuManager\n        self.menu_manager = MenuManager(self)\n        \n        # Setup window manager\n        from src.window_manager_pyqt5 import WindowManager\n        self.window_manager = WindowManager()\n        \n        self.setup_ui()\n        \n        # Timer for clock updates\n        self.timer = QTimer()\n        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_time)\n        self.timer.start(1000)\n        \n        # Timer for window list updates\n        self.window_timer = QTimer()\n        self.window_timer.timeout.connect(self.update_window_list)\n        self.window_timer.start(2000)  # Update every 2 seconds\n    \n    def setup_ui(self):\n        \"\"\"Setup the panel UI\"\"\"\n        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(5, 2, 5, 2)\n        layout.setSpacing(5)\n        \n        # Left side: Menus button with dynamic menu\n        self.menus_button = QPushButton(\"\u2630\")\n        self.menus_button.setFixedWidth(30)\n        self.menus_button.setMenu(self.build_menus_menu())\n        layout.addWidget(self.menus_button)\n        \n        # Window switcher dropdown\n        self.window_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.window_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\")\n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(self.window_manager.is_available())\n        self.window_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_window_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.window_combo)\n        \n        # Desktop buttons\n        self.desktop_group = QButtonGroup(self)\n        self.desktop_buttons = []\n        for i in range(4):\n            btn = QPushButton(str(i + 1))\n            btn.setFixedWidth(25)\n            btn.setFixedHeight(22)\n            btn.setCheckable(True)\n            btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked, d=i: self.switch_desktop(d))\n            self.desktop_group.addButton(btn)\n            self.desktop_buttons.append(btn)\n            layout.addWidget(btn)\n        \n        # Middle: Gadgets frame\n        self.gadgets_frame = QFrame()\n        self.gadgets_frame.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: transparent;\")\n        self.gadgets_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.gadgets_frame)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setSpacing(5)\n        layout.addWidget(self.gadgets_frame)\n        \n        layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # Clock\n        self.time_label = QLabel(\"00:00\")\n        self.time_label.setFont(QFont(\"Arial\", 10, QFont.Bold))\n        layout.addWidget(self.time_label)\n        \n        # Config dropdown\n        self.config_combo = QComboBox()\n        # Config dropdown\n        self.config_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.config_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\u2699 Config\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Manage Gadgets\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Panel Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Menu Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Date/Time Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"About KosDWM\")\n        self.config_combo.setFixedWidth(120)\n        self.config_combo.setFixedHeight(22)\n        self.config_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_config_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.config_combo)\n        \n        # Load gadgets\n        self.load_gadgets()\n        \n        # Set current desktop\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        \n        # Initial window list population\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_window_list(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the window switcher dropdown with current windows\"\"\"\n        if not self.window_manager.is_available():\n            self.window_combo.setEnabled(False)\n            return\n        \n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(True)\n        \n        # Get current selection before clearing\n        current_id = None\n        current_index = self.window_combo.currentIndex()\n        if current_index > 0:  # 0 is placeholder\n            current_data = self.window_combo.itemData(current_index)\n            if current_data:\n                current_id = current_data.get('id')\n        \n        # Block signals while updating\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.window_combo.clear()\n        \n        # Add placeholder with icon\n        self.window_combo.addItem(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\", None)\n        \n        # Get windows and add to dropdown\n        windows = self.window_manager.get_windows()\n        selected_index = 0\n        \n        for i, window in enumerate(windows):\n            # Truncate long titles\n            title = window['title']\n            display_title = title[:32] + \"...\" if len(title) > 35 else title\n            \n            # Add desktop number prefix if not on current desktop\n            display_text = display_title\n            desktop_prefix = \"\"\n            if window['desktop'] != -1:  # -1 means sticky/all desktops\n                current_desktop = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n                if window['desktop'] != current_desktop:\n                    desktop_prefix = f\"[{window['desktop']+1}] \"\n                    display_text = f\"{desktop_prefix}{display_title}\"\n            \n            index = self.window_combo.count()\n            self.window_combo.addItem(display_text, window)\n            \n            # Set tooltip with full info\n            tooltip = f\"{desktop_prefix}{window['name']}\\nDesktop: {window['desktop']+1 if window['desktop'] >= 0 else 'All'}\"\n            self.window_combo.setItemData(index, tooltip, Qt.ToolTipRole)\n            \n            # Restore selection if this window was selected\n            if current_id and window['id'] == current_id:\n                selected_index = index\n        \n        # Restore selection\n        self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(selected_index)\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(False)\n    \n    def on_window_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle window selection from dropdown\"\"\"\n        if index <= 0:  # Placeholder selected\n            return\n        \n        window = self.window_combo.itemData(index)\n        if window and 'id' in window:\n            # Visual feedback - briefly highlight the combo\n            self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QComboBox {\n                    background-color: #666666;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 5px;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                    min-width: 120px;\n                    max-width: 200px;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            \n            # Activate the window\n            success = self.window_manager.activate_window(window['id'])\n            \n            # Reset to placeholder after activation\n            self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            \n            # Reset style after a short delay\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer\n            QTimer.singleShot(200, self._reset_window_combo_style)\n    \n    def _reset_window_combo_style(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset window combo to normal style\"\"\"\n        self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    def load_gadgets(self):\n        \"\"\"Load gadgets into the panel\"\"\"\n        from src.gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetManager\n        \n        # Clear existing gadgets\n        while self.gadgets_layout.count():\n            item = self.gadgets_layout.takeAt(0)\n            if item.widget():\n                item.widget().deleteLater()\n        \n        # Create gadget manager\n        self.gadget_manager = GadgetManager()\n        \n        # Store gadget->button mapping for refresh\n        self._gadget_buttons = {}\n        \n        # Add enabled gadgets\n        for gadget in self.gadget_manager.get_enabled_gadgets():\n            btn = QPushButton(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setFixedSize(40, 22)\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n            # Store gadget reference on button to avoid lambda capture issues\n            btn._gadget = gadget\n            btn.clicked.connect(self._on_gadget_clicked)\n            self.gadgets_layout.addWidget(btn)\n            # Store mapping\n            self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()] = btn\n            # Set panel reference on gadget for refresh notifications\n            gadget.set_panel(self)\n    \n    def refresh_gadget_icon(self, gadget):\n        \"\"\"Refresh icon for a specific gadget\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, '_gadget_buttons') and gadget.get_name() in self._gadget_buttons:\n            btn = self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()]\n            btn.setText(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n    \n    def _on_gadget_clicked(self):\n        \"\"\"Handle gadget button click\"\"\"\n        btn = self.sender()\n        if btn and hasattr(btn, '_gadget'):\n            try:\n                btn._gadget.on_click()\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in gadget click: {e}\")\n                import traceback\n                traceback.print_exc()\n    \n    def update_time(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the clock\"\"\"\n        current = datetime.now().strftime(\"%H:%M\")\n        self.time_label.setText(current)\n    \n    def switch_desktop(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Switch to specified desktop\"\"\"\n        self.desktop_manager.switch_to_desktop(desktop_id)\n        # Update window list to show new desktop's windows\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def on_desktop_changed(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Handle desktop change\"\"\"\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_desktop_buttons(self):\n        \"\"\"Update desktop button states\"\"\"\n        current = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n        for i, btn in enumerate(self.desktop_buttons):\n            btn.setChecked(i == current)\n            if i == current:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #666666; font-weight: bold;\")\n            else:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #4a4a4a;\")\n    \n    def build_menus_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Build the dynamic menus\"\"\"\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        \n        # Light theme for menu\n        menu.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QMenu {\n                background-color: #f5f5f5;\n                color: #333333;\n                border: 1px solid #cccccc;\n                padding: 5px;\n            }\n            QMenu::item {\n                padding: 8px 25px;\n                border-radius: 3px;\n                color: #333333;\n            }\n            QMenu::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: white;\n            }\n            QMenu::separator {\n                height: 1px;\n                background-color: #cccccc;\n                margin: 5px 0px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        # Load dynamic menus from directory structure\n        for menu_data in self.menu_manager.load_menus():\n            if menu_data.get(\"type\") == \"branch\":\n                # Create top-level submenu\n                submenu = menu.addMenu(menu_data[\"name\"])\n                self.menu_manager._populate_menu(submenu, menu_data.get(\"items\", []))\n        \n        menu.addSeparator()\n        menu.addAction(\"Exit\", self.window().close)\n        \n        return menu\n    \n    def on_config_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle configuration dropdown selection\"\"\"\n        if index < 0:\n            return\n        \n        # Block signals temporarily to prevent re-triggering when resetting\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.config_combo.setCurrentIndex(-1)\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(False)\n        \n        # Open appropriate dialog\n        if index == 0:\n            self.open_gadget_config()\n        elif index == 1:\n            self.open_panel_config()\n        elif index == 2:\n            self.open_menu_config()\n        elif index == 3:\n            self.open_datetime_config()\n        elif index == 4:\n            self.open_about_dialog()\n    \n    def open_gadget_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open gadget configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.gadget_config_pyqt5 import GadgetConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = GadgetConfigDialog(self.gadget_manager, self)\n        if dialog.exec_():\n            self.load_gadgets()\n    \n    def open_panel_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open panel configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.panel_config_pyqt5 import PanelConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = PanelConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_menu_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open menu configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.menu_config_pyqt5 import MenuConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = MenuConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_datetime_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open date/time configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.datetime_config_pyqt5 import DateTimeConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = DateTimeConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n\n\n\n    def open_about_dialog(self):\n        \"\"\"Open About KosDWM dialog\"\"\"\n        from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton, QHBoxLayout\n        from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt\n        import sys\n        import platform\n        \n        # Get version\n        try:\n            from AUTOVERSION import VERSION as KOSDWM_VERSION\n        except ImportError:\n            KOSDWM_VERSION = \"unknown\"\n        \n        dialog = QDialog(self)\n        dialog.setWindowTitle(\"About KosDWM\")\n        dialog.setGeometry(100, 100, 400, 300)\n        dialog.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QDialog {\n                background-color: #1a1a1a;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: #ffffff;\n                font-size: 13px;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: #ffffff;\n                border: 1px solid #555555;\n                padding: 8px 16px;\n                border-radius: 4px;\n                font-weight: bold;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #5a5a5a;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        layout = QVBoxLayout(dialog)\n        layout.setSpacing(15)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)\n        \n        # Title\n        title = QLabel(\"KosDWM\")\n        title.setStyleSheet(\"font-size: 24px; font-weight: bold; color: #4a90d9;\")\n        title.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(title)\n        \n        # Version\n        version = QLabel(f\"Version {KOSDWM_VERSION}\")\n        version.setStyleSheet(\"font-size: 14px; color: #aaaaaa;\")\n        version.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(version)\n        \n        # Separator\n        line = QLabel(\"\u2500\" * 40)\n        line.setStyleSheet(\"color: #444444;\")\n        line.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(line)\n        \n        # Description\n        desc = QLabel(\"A dynamic window manager with Python-based gadget system\")\n        desc.setWordWrap(True)\n        desc.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(desc)\n        \n        # Credits\n        credits = QLabel(\"\"\"\n<b>Created by:</b><br>\nB.K. <!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nGadget Configuration Dialog for KosDWM PyQt5\n\nConfiguration dialog for enabling/disabling gadgets\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom PyQt5.QtWidgets import QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, QCheckBox\nfrom PyQt5.QtWidgets import QPushButton, QScrollArea, QWidget, QFrame, QMessageBox\nfrom PyQt5.QtCore import Qt\n\n\nclass GadgetConfigDialog(QDialog):\n    \"\"\"\n    Configuration dialog for enabling/disabling gadgets\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, gadget_manager, parent=None):\n        super().__init__(parent)\n        self.gadget_manager = gadget_manager\n        self.checkboxes = {}\n        \n        self.setWindowTitle(\"Gadget Configuration\")\n        self.setGeometry(100, 100, 600, 500)\n        \n        # Dark theme styling\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QDialog {\n                background-color: #1a1a1a;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: #ffffff;\n            }\n            QLabel#header {\n                font-size: 18px;\n                font-weight: bold;\n                color: #ffffff;\n            }\n            QLabel#description {\n                color: #aaaaaa;\n                font-size: 13px;\n            }\n            QCheckBox {\n                color: #ffffff;\n                spacing: 8px;\n                font-size: 14px;\n            }\n            QCheckBox::indicator {\n                width: 18px;\n                height: 18px;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: #ffffff;\n                border: 1px solid #555555;\n                padding: 10px 20px;\n                border-radius: 5px;\n                font-size: 14px;\n                font-weight: bold;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #5a5a5a;\n            }\n            QPushButton:pressed {\n                background-color: #6a6a6a;\n            }\n            QScrollArea {\n                border: 1px solid #444444;\n                background-color: #2d2d2d;\n            }\n            QFrame#gadgetItem {\n                background-color: #2d2d2d;\n                border: 1px solid #444444;\n                border-radius: 5px;\n                padding: 10px;\n            }\n            QFrame#gadgetItem:hover {\n                background-color: #3d3d3d;\n                border-color: #555555;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        self.setup_ui()\n    \n    def setup_ui(self):\n        \"\"\"Setup the dialog UI\"\"\"\n        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)\n        layout.setSpacing(15)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)\n        \n        # Header\n        header = QLabel(\"\u2699\ufe0f Gadget Configuration\")\n        header.setObjectName(\"header\")\n        layout.addWidget(header)\n        \n        # Description\n        desc = QLabel(\"Enable or disable gadgets to customize your panel\")\n        desc.setObjectName(\"description\")\n        desc.setWordWrap(True)\n        layout.addWidget(desc)\n        \n        # Scroll area for gadgets\n        scroll = QScrollArea()\n        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)\n        scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)\n        \n        # Container for gadgets\n        container = QWidget()\n        container_layout = QVBoxLayout(container)\n        container_layout.setSpacing(10)\n        container_layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignTop)\n        \n        # Get available gadgets\n        available = self.gadget_manager.get_all_gadgets()\n        \n        if not available:\n            label = QLabel(\"No gadgets available\")\n            label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n            label.setStyleSheet(\"color: #888888; font-style: italic; padding: 50px;\")\n            container_layout.addWidget(label)\n        else:\n            for gadget_name in available:\n                info = self.gadget_manager.get_gadget_info(gadget_name)\n                if info:\n                    self._create_gadget_item(container_layout, info)\n        \n        container_layout.addStretch()\n        scroll.setWidget(container)\n        layout.addWidget(scroll)\n        \n        # Buttons\n        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()\n        \n        reload_btn = QPushButton(\"\ud83d\udd04 Reload\")\n        reload_btn.setToolTip(\"Reload gadgets from disk\")\n        reload_btn.clicked.connect(self.reload_gadgets)\n        btn_layout.addWidget(reload_btn)\n        \n        btn_layout.addStretch()\n        \n        cancel_btn = QPushButton(\"Cancel\")\n        cancel_btn.clicked.connect(self.reject)\n        btn_layout.addWidget(cancel_btn)\n        \n        save_btn = QPushButton(\"\ud83d\udcbe Save\")\n        save_btn.setDefault(True)\n        save_btn.clicked.connect(self.save_config)\n        btn_layout.addWidget(save_btn)\n        \n        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)\n    \n    def _create_gadget_item(self, layout, info):\n        \"\"\"Create a widget for a single gadget\"\"\"\n        frame = QFrame()\n        frame.setObjectName(\"gadgetItem\")\n        frame_layout = QHBoxLayout(frame)\n        frame_layout.setSpacing(10)\n        \n        # Checkbox for enabling\n        checkbox = QCheckBox(f\"{info['icon']} {info['name']}\")\n        checkbox.setChecked(info['enabled'])\n        checkbox.setToolTip(info['description'])\n        self.checkboxes[info['name']] = checkbox\n        frame_layout.addWidget(checkbox)\n        \n        frame_layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # Source label\n        source = info.get('source', 'unknown')\n        source_label = QLabel(f\"[{source}]\")\n        source_label.setStyleSheet(\"color: #888888; font-size: 11px;\")\n        frame_layout.addWidget(source_label)\n        \n        layout.addWidget(frame)\n        \n        # Description below\n        if info.get('description'):\n            desc = QLabel(info['description'])\n            desc.setObjectName(\"description\")\n            desc.setWordWrap(True)\n            desc.setIndent(30)  # Align with checkbox\n            layout.addWidget(desc)\n    \n    def save_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Save the configuration\"\"\"\n        for gadget_name, checkbox in self.checkboxes.items():\n            if checkbox.isChecked():\n                self.gadget_manager.enable_gadget(gadget_name)\n            else:\n                self.gadget_manager.disable_gadget(gadget_name)\n        \n        self.accept()\n    \n    def reload_gadgets(self):\n        \"\"\"Reload gadgets from disk\"\"\"\n        reply = QMessageBox.question(\n            self,\n            \"Reload Gadgets\",\n            \"This will reload all gadgets from disk. Continue?\",\n            QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No\n        )\n        \n        if reply == QMessageBox.Yes:\n            self.gadget_manager.reload_gadgets()\n            # Refresh UI\n            self.close()\n            dialog = GadgetConfigDialog(self.gadget_manager, self.parent())\n            dialog.exec_()\n", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 213, "timestamp": 1784915096.1433663, "date": "2026-07-24", "name": "ReadFile"} -->

font-size: 13px;\")\n        version.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(version)\n        \n        # Separator line\n        separator = QLabel(\"\u2500\" * 30)\n        separator.setStyleSheet(\"color: #444444;\")\n        separator.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(separator)\n        \n        # Description\n        desc = QLabel(\n            \"A dynamic window manager with Python-based gadget system.\\n\"\n            \"Designed for flexibility and extensibility.\"\n        )\n        desc.setWordWrap(True)\n        desc.setStyleSheet(\"color: #cccccc; font-size: 12px;\")\n        desc.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(desc)\n        \n        # Separator\n        separator2 = QLabel(\"\u2500\" * 30)\n        separator2.setStyleSheet(\"color: #444444;\")\n        separator2.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(separator2)\n        \n        # Credits section\n        credits_label = QLabel(\"<b>Credits</b>\")\n        credits_label.setStyleSheet(\"color: #ffffff; font-size: 13px;\")\n        credits_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(credits_label)\n        \n        credits = QLabel(\n            \"<b>Created by:</b> B.K. (kosumosu)<br><br>\"\n            \"<b>Contributors:</b><br>\"\n            \"OpenCode (editor + BigPickle)<br>\"\n            \"Ollama models\"\n        )\n        credits.setWordWrap(True)\n        credits.setStyleSheet(\"color: #cccccc; font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.4;\")\n        credits.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(credits)\n        \n        layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # System Info\n        sys_info = QLabel(self.get_system_info())\n        sys_info.setWordWrap(True)\n        sys_info.setStyleSheet(\"color: #888888; font-size: 10px;\")\n        sys_info.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(sys_info)\n        \n        # OK button\n        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()\n        btn_layout.addStretch()\n        \n        ok_btn = QPushButton(\"OK\")\n        ok_btn.setFixedWidth(80)\n        ok_btn.clicked.connect(self.accept)\n        btn_layout.addWidget(ok_btn)\n        \n        btn_layout.addStretch()\n        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)\n    \n    def get_version(self):\n        \"\"\"Get KosDWM version from AUTOVERSION\"\"\"\n        try:\n            import sys\n            from pathlib import Path\n            # Look for AUTOVERSION.py in parent of src directory\n            current = Path(__file__).parent\n            version_file = current.parent / \"AUTOVERSION.py\"\n            if version_file.exists():\n                namespace = {}\n                exec(open(version_file).read(), namespace)\n                return namespace.get(\"VERSION\", \"unknown\")\n            return \"unknown\"\n        except Exception:\n            return \"unknown\"\n    \n    def get_system_info(self):\n        \"\"\"Get system information string\"\"\"\n        try:\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QT_VERSION_STR\n            py_version = platform.python_version()\n            qt_version = QT_VERSION_STR\n            plat = f\"{platform.system()} {platform.release()}\"\n            return f\"Python {py_version} | Qt {qt_version} | {plat}\"\n        except Exception:\n            return f\"Python {platform.python_version()}\"\n    \n    def apply_dark_theme(self):\n        \"\"\"Apply dark theme styling consistent with other dialogs\"\"\"\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QDialog {\n                background-color: #1a1a1a;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: #ffffff;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: #ffffff;\n                border: 1px solid #555555;\n                padding: 8px 16px;\n                border-radius: 4px;\n                font-weight: bold;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #5a5a5a;\n            }\n            QPushButton:pressed {\n                background-color: #6a6a6a;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n</contentOfFile>\n</WriteFile>", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 213, "timestamp": 1784915117.90764, "date": "2026-07-24", "thinking": "", "prompt_tokens": 12994, "response_tokens": 1466} -->
