Height is controlled by parent window (30px)\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QFrame {\n                background-color: #333333;\n                border: none;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 8px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #555555;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: white;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        # Setup desktop manager\n        from src.desktop_manager_pyqt5 import DesktopManager\n        self.desktop_manager = DesktopManager(self)\n        self.desktop_manager.desktop_changed.connect(self.on_desktop_changed)\n        \n        # Setup menu manager\n        from src.menus_pyqt5 import MenuManager\n        self.menu_manager = MenuManager(self)\n        \n        # Setup window manager\n        from src.window_manager_pyqt5 import WindowManager\n        self.window_manager = WindowManager()\n        \n        self.setup_ui()\n        \n        # Timer for clock updates\n        self.timer = QTimer()\n        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_time)\n        self.timer.start(1000)\n        \n        # Timer for window list updates\n        self.window_timer = QTimer()\n        self.window_timer.timeout.connect(self.update_window_list)\n        self.window_timer.start(2000)  # Update every 2 seconds\n    \n    def setup_ui(self):\n        \"\"\"Setup the panel UI\"\"\"\n        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(5, 2, 5, 2)\n        layout.setSpacing(5)\n        \n        # Left side: Menus button with dynamic menu\n        self.menus_button = QPushButton(\"\u2630\")\n        self.menus_button.setFixedWidth(30)\n        self.menus_button.setMenu(self.build_menus_menu())\n        layout.addWidget(self.menus_button)\n        \n        # Window switcher dropdown\n        self.window_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.window_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\")\n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(self.window_manager.is_available())\n        self.window_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_window_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.window_combo)\n        \n        # Desktop buttons\n        self.desktop_group = QButtonGroup(self)\n        self.desktop_buttons = []\n        for i in range(4):\n            btn = QPushButton(str(i + 1))\n            btn.setFixedWidth(25)\n            btn.setFixedHeight(22)\n            btn.setCheckable(True)\n            btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked, d=i: self.switch_desktop(d))\n            self.desktop_group.addButton(btn)\n            self.desktop_buttons.append(btn)\n            layout.addWidget(btn)\n        \n        # Middle: Gadgets frame\n        self.gadgets_frame = QFrame()\n        self.gadgets_frame.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: transparent;\")\n        self.gadgets_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.gadgets_frame)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setSpacing(5)\n        layout.addWidget(self.gadgets_frame)\n        \n        layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # Clock\n        self.time_label = QLabel(\"00:00\")\n        self.time_label.setFont(QFont(\"Arial\", 10, QFont.Bold))\n        layout.addWidget(self.time_label)\n        \n        # Config dropdown\n        self.config_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.config_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\u2699 Config\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Manage Gadgets\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Panel Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Menu Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Date/Time Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"About KosDWM\")\n        self.config_combo.setFixedWidth(120)\n        self.config_combo.setFixedHeight(22)\n        self.config_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_config_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.config_combo)\n        \n        # Load gadgets\n        self.load_gadgets()\n        \n        # Set current desktop\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        \n        # Initial window list population\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_window_list(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the window switcher dropdown with current windows\"\"\"\n        if not self.window_manager.is_available():\n            self.window_combo.setEnabled(False)\n            return\n        \n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(True)\n        \n        # Get current selection before clearing\n        current_id = None\n        current_index = self.window_combo.currentIndex()\n        if current_index > 0:  # 0 is placeholder\n            current_data = self.window_combo.itemData(current_index)\n            if current_data:\n                current_id = current_data.get('id')\n        \n        # Block signals while updating\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.window_combo.clear()\n        \n        # Add placeholder with icon\n        self.window_combo.addItem(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\", None)\n        \n        # Get windows and add to dropdown\n        windows = self.window_manager.get_windows()\n        selected_index = 0\n        \n        for i, window in enumerate(windows):\n            # Truncate long titles\n            title = window['title']\n            display_title = title[:32] + \"...\" if len(title) > 35 else title\n            \n            # Add desktop number prefix if not on current desktop\n            display_text = display_title\n            desktop_prefix = \"\"\n            if window['desktop'] != -1:  # -1 means sticky/all desktops\n                current_desktop = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n                if window['desktop'] != current_desktop:\n                    desktop_prefix = f\"[{window['desktop']+1}] \"\n                    display_text = f\"{desktop_prefix}{display_title}\"\n            \n            index = self.window_combo.count()\n            self.window_combo.addItem(display_text, window)\n            \n            # Set tooltip with full info\n            tooltip = f\"{desktop_prefix}{window['name']}\\nDesktop: {window['desktop']+1 if window['desktop'] >= 0 else 'All'}\"\n            self.window_combo.setItemData(index, tooltip, Qt.ToolTipRole)\n            \n            # Restore selection if this window was selected\n            if current_id and window['id'] == current_id:\n                selected_index = index\n        \n        # Restore selection\n        self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(selected_index)\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(False)\n    \n    def on_window_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle window selection from dropdown\"\"\"\n        if index <= 0:  # Placeholder selected\n            return\n        \n        window = self.window_combo.itemData(index)\n        if window and 'id' in window:\n            # Visual feedback - briefly highlight the combo\n            self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QComboBox {\n                    background-color: #666666;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 5px;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                    min-width: 120px;\n                    max-width: 200px;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            \n            # Activate the window\n            success = self.window_manager.activate_window(window['id'])\n            \n            # Reset to placeholder after activation\n            self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            \n            # Reset style after a short delay\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer\n            QTimer.singleShot(200, self._reset_window_combo_style)\n    \n    def _reset_window_combo_style(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset window combo to normal style\"\"\"\n        self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    \n    def load_gadgets(self):\n        \"\"\"Load gadgets into the panel\"\"\"\n        from src.gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetManager\n        \n        # Clear existing gadgets\n        while self.gadgets_layout.count():\n            item = self.gadgets_layout.takeAt(0)\n            if item.widget():\n                item.widget().deleteLater()\n        \n        # Create gadget manager\n        self.gadget_manager = GadgetManager()\n        \n        # Store gadget->button mapping for refresh\n        self._gadget_buttons = {}\n        \n        # Add enabled gadgets\n        for gadget in self.gadget_manager.get_enabled_gadgets():\n            btn = QPushButton(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setFixedSize(40, 22)\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n            # Store gadget reference on button to avoid lambda capture issues\n            btn._gadget = gadget\n            btn.clicked.connect(self._on_gadget_clicked)\n            self.gadgets_layout.addWidget(btn)\n            # Store mapping\n            self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()] = btn\n            # Set panel reference on gadget for refresh notifications\n            gadget.set_panel(self)\n    \n    def refresh_gadget_icon(self, gadget):\n        \"\"\"Refresh icon for a specific gadget\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, '_gadget_buttons') and gadget.get_name() in self._gadget_buttons:\n            btn = self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()]\n            btn.setText(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n    \n    def _on_gadget_clicked(self):\n        \"\"\"Handle gadget button click\"\"\"\n        btn = self.sender()\n        if btn and hasattr(btn, '_gadget'):\n            try:\n                btn._gadget.on_click()\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in gadget click: {e}\")\n                import traceback\n                traceback.print_exc()\n    \n    def update_time(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the clock\"\"\"\n        current = datetime.now().strftime(\"%H:%M\")\n        self.time_label.setText(current)\n    \n    def switch_desktop(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Switch to specified desktop\"\"\"\n        self.desktop_manager.switch_to_desktop(desktop_id)\n        # Update window list to show new desktop's windows\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def on_desktop_changed(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Handle desktop change\"\"\"\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_desktop_buttons(self):\n        \"\"\"Update desktop button states\"\"\"\n        current = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n        for i, btn in enumerate(self.desktop_buttons):\n            btn.setChecked(i == current)\n            if i == current:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #666666; font-weight: bold;\")\n            else:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #4a4a4a;\")\n    \n    def build_menus_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Build the dynamic menus\"\"\"\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        \n        # Light theme for menu\n        menu.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QMenu {\n                background-color: #f5f5f5;\n                color: #333333;\n                border: 1px solid #cccccc;\n                padding: 5px;\n            }\n            QMenu::item {\n                padding: 8px 25px;\n                border-radius: 3px;\n                color: #333333;\n            }\n            QMenu::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: white;\n            }\n            QMenu::separator {\n                height: 1px;\n                background-color: #cccccc;\n                margin: 5px 0px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        # Load dynamic menus from directory structure\n        for menu_data in self.menu_manager.load_menus():\n            if menu_data.get(\"type\") == \"branch\":\n                # Create top-level submenu\n                submenu = menu.addMenu(menu_data[\"name\"])\n                self.menu_manager._populate_menu(submenu, menu_data.get(\"items\", []))\n        \n        menu.addSeparator()\n        menu.addAction(\"Exit\", self.window().close)\n        \n        return menu\n    \n    def on_config_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle configuration dropdown selection\"\"\"\n        if index < 0:\n            return\n        \n        # Block signals temporarily to prevent re-triggering when resetting\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.config_combo.setCurrentIndex(-1)\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(False)\n        \n        # Open appropriate dialog\n        if index == 0:\n            self.open_gadget_config()\n        elif index == 1:\n            self.open_panel_config()\n        elif index == 2:\n            self.open_menu_config()\n        elif index == 3:\n            self.open_datetime_config()\n        elif index == 4:\n            self.open_about_dialog()\n    \n    def open_gadget_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open gadget configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.gadget_config_pyqt5 import GadgetConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = GadgetConfigDialog(self.gadget_manager, self)\n        if dialog.exec_():\n            self.load_gadgets()\n    \n    def open_panel_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open panel configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.panel_config_pyqt5 import PanelConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = PanelConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_menu_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open menu configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.menu_config_pyqt5 import MenuConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = MenuConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_datetime_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open date/time configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.datetime_config_pyqt5 import DateTimeConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = DateTimeConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_about_dialog(self):\n        \"\"\"Open About KosDWM dialog\"\"\"\n        from src.about_dialog_pyqt5 import AboutDialog\n        \n        dialog = AboutDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 215, "timestamp": 1784915187.077034, "date": "2026-07-24", "name": "ReadFile"} -->

font-size: 13px;\")\n        version.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(version)\n        \n        # Separator line\n        separator = QLabel(\"\u2500\" * 30)\n        separator.setStyleSheet(\"color: #444444;\")\n        separator.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(separator)\n        \n        # Description\n        desc = QLabel(\n            \"A dynamic window manager with Python-based gadget system.\\n\"\n            \"Designed for flexibility and extensibility.\"\n        )\n        desc.setWordWrap(True)\n        desc.setStyleSheet(\"color: #cccccc; font-size: 12px;\")\n        desc.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(desc)\n        \n        # Separator\n        separator2 = QLabel(\"\u2500\" * 30)\n        separator2.setStyleSheet(\"color: #444444;\")\n        separator2.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(separator2)\n        \n        # Credits section\n        credits_label = QLabel(\"<b>Credits</b>\")\n        credits_label.setStyleSheet(\"color: #ffffff; font-size: 13px;\")\n        credits_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(credits_label)\n        \n        credits = QLabel(\n            \"<b>Created by:</b> B.K. (kosumosu)<br><br>\"\n            \"<b>Contributors:</b><br>\"\n            \"OpenCode (editor + BigPickle)<br>\"\n            \"Ollama models\"\n        )\n        credits.setWordWrap(True)\n        credits.setStyleSheet(\"color: #cccccc; font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.4;\")\n        credits.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)\n        layout.addWidget(credits)\n        \n        layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # OK button\n        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()\n        btn_layout.addStretch()\n        \n        ok_btn = QPushButton(\"OK\")\n        ok_btn.setFixedWidth(80)\n        ok_btn.clicked.connect(self.accept)\n        btn_layout.addWidget(ok_btn)\n        \n        btn_layout.addStretch()\n        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)\n    \n    def get_version(self):\n        \"\"\"Get KosDWM version from AUTOVERSION\"\"\"\n        try:\n            import sys\n            from pathlib import Path\n            # Look for AUTOVERSION.py in parent of src directory\n            current = Path(__file__).parent\n            version_file = current.parent / \"AUTOVERSION.py\"\n            if version_file.exists():\n                namespace = {}\n                exec(open(version_file).read(), namespace)\n                return namespace.get(\"VERSION\", \"unknown\")\n            return \"unknown\"\n        except Exception:\n            return \"unknown\"\n    \n    def get_system_info(self):\n        \"\"\"Get system information string\"\"\"\n        try:\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QT_VERSION_STR\n            py_version = platform.python_version()\n            qt_version = QT_VERSION_STR\n            plat = f\"{platform.system()} {platform.release()}\"\n            return f\"Python {py_version} | Qt {qt_version} | {plat}\"\n        except Exception:\n            return f\"Python {platform.python_version()}\"\n    \n    def apply_dark_theme(self):\n        \"\"\"Apply dark theme styling consistent with other dialogs\"\"\"\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QDialog {\n                background-color: #1a1a1a;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: #ffffff;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: #ffffff;\n                border: 1px solid #555555;\n                padding: 8px 16px;\n                border-radius: 4px;\n                font-weight: bold;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #5a5a5a;\n            }\n            QPushButton:pressed {\n                background-color: #6a6a6a;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 215, "timestamp": 1784915191.9207165, "date": "2026-07-24", "name": "ReadFile"} -->

Height is controlled by parent window (30px)\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QFrame {\n                background-color: #333333;\n                border: none;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 8px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #555555;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: white;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        # Setup desktop manager\n        from src.desktop_manager_pyqt5 import DesktopManager\n        self.desktop_manager = DesktopManager(self)\n        self.desktop_manager.desktop_changed.connect(self.on_desktop_changed)\n        \n        # Setup menu manager\n        from src.menus_pyqt5 import MenuManager\n        self.menu_manager = MenuManager(self)\n        \n        # Setup window manager\n        from src.window_manager_pyqt5 import WindowManager\n        self.window_manager = WindowManager()\n        \n        self.setup_ui()\n        \n        # Timer for clock updates\n        self.timer = QTimer()\n        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_time)\n        self.timer.start(1000)\n        \n        # Timer for window list updates\n        self.window_timer = QTimer()\n        self.window_timer.timeout.connect(self.update_window_list)\n        self.window_timer.start(2000)  # Update every 2 seconds\n    \n    def setup_ui(self):\n        \"\"\"Setup the panel UI\"\"\"\n        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(5, 2, 5, 2)\n        layout.setSpacing(5)\n        \n        # Left side: Menus button with dynamic menu\n        self.menus_button = QPushButton(\"\u2630\")\n        self.menus_button.setFixedWidth(30)\n        self.menus_button.setMenu(self.build_menus_menu())\n        layout.addWidget(self.menus_button)\n        \n        # Window switcher dropdown\n        self.window_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.window_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\")\n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(self.window_manager.is_available())\n        self.window_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_window_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.window_combo)\n        \n        # Desktop buttons\n        self.desktop_group = QButtonGroup(self)\n        self.desktop_buttons = []\n        for i in range(4):\n            btn = QPushButton(str(i + 1))\n            btn.setFixedWidth(25)\n            btn.setFixedHeight(22)\n            btn.setCheckable(True)\n            btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked, d=i: self.switch_desktop(d))\n            self.desktop_group.addButton(btn)\n            self.desktop_buttons.append(btn)\n            layout.addWidget(btn)\n        \n        # Middle: Gadgets frame\n        self.gadgets_frame = QFrame()\n        self.gadgets_frame.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: transparent;\")\n        self.gadgets_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.gadgets_frame)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setSpacing(5)\n        layout.addWidget(self.gadgets_frame)\n        \n        layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # Clock\n        self.time_label = QLabel(\"00:00\")\n        self.time_label.setFont(QFont(\"Arial\", 10, QFont.Bold))\n        layout.addWidget(self.time_label)\n        \n        # Config dropdown\n        self.config_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.config_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\u2699 Config\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Manage Gadgets\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Panel Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Menu Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Date/Time Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"About KosDWM\")\n        self.config_combo.setFixedWidth(120)\n        self.config_combo.setFixedHeight(22)\n        self.config_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_config_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.config_combo)\n        \n        # Load gadgets\n        self.load_gadgets()\n        \n        # Set current desktop\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        \n        # Initial window list population\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_window_list(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the window switcher dropdown with current windows\"\"\"\n        if not self.window_manager.is_available():\n            self.window_combo.setEnabled(False)\n            return\n        \n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(True)\n        \n        # Get current selection before clearing\n        current_id = None\n        current_index = self.window_combo.currentIndex()\n        if current_index > 0:  # 0 is placeholder\n            current_data = self.window_combo.itemData(current_index)\n            if current_data:\n                current_id = current_data.get('id')\n        \n        # Block signals while updating\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.window_combo.clear()\n        \n        # Add placeholder with icon\n        self.window_combo.addItem(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\", None)\n        \n        # Get windows and add to dropdown\n        windows = self.window_manager.get_windows()\n        selected_index = 0\n        \n        for i, window in enumerate(windows):\n            # Truncate long titles\n            title = window['title']\n            display_title = title[:32] + \"...\" if len(title) > 35 else title\n            \n            # Add desktop number prefix if not on current desktop\n            display_text = display_title\n            desktop_prefix = \"\"\n            if window['desktop'] != -1:  # -1 means sticky/all desktops\n                current_desktop = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n                if window['desktop'] != current_desktop:\n                    desktop_prefix = f\"[{window['desktop']+1}] \"\n                    display_text = f\"{desktop_prefix}{display_title}\"\n            \n            index = self.window_combo.count()\n            self.window_combo.addItem(display_text, window)\n            \n            # Set tooltip with full info\n            tooltip = f\"{desktop_prefix}{window['name']}\\nDesktop: {window['desktop']+1 if window['desktop'] >= 0 else 'All'}\"\n            self.window_combo.setItemData(index, tooltip, Qt.ToolTipRole)\n            \n            # Restore selection if this window was selected\n            if current_id and window['id'] == current_id:\n                selected_index = index\n        \n        # Restore selection\n        self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(selected_index)\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(False)\n    \n    def on_window_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle window selection from dropdown\"\"\"\n        if index <= 0:  # Placeholder selected\n            return\n        \n        window = self.window_combo.itemData(index)\n        if window and 'id' in window:\n            # Visual feedback - briefly highlight the combo\n            self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QComboBox {\n                    background-color: #666666;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 5px;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                    min-width: 120px;\n                    max-width: 200px;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            \n            # Activate the window\n            success = self.window_manager.activate_window(window['id'])\n            \n            # Reset to placeholder after activation\n            self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            \n            # Reset style after a short delay\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer\n            QTimer.singleShot(200, self._reset_window_combo_style)\n    \n    def _reset_window_combo_style(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset window combo to normal style\"\"\"\n        self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    \n    def load_gadgets(self):\n        \"\"\"Load gadgets into the panel\"\"\"\n        from src.gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetManager\n        \n        # Clear existing gadgets\n        while self.gadgets_layout.count():\n            item = self.gadgets_layout.takeAt(0)\n            if item.widget():\n                item.widget().deleteLater()\n        \n        # Create gadget manager\n        self.gadget_manager = GadgetManager()\n        \n        # Store gadget->button mapping for refresh\n        self._gadget_buttons = {}\n        \n        # Add enabled gadgets\n        for gadget in self.gadget_manager.get_enabled_gadgets():\n            btn = QPushButton(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setFixedSize(40, 22)\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n            # Store gadget reference on button to avoid lambda capture issues\n            btn._gadget = gadget\n            btn.clicked.connect(self._on_gadget_clicked)\n            self.gadgets_layout.addWidget(btn)\n            # Store mapping\n            self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()] = btn\n            # Set panel reference on gadget for refresh notifications\n            gadget.set_panel(self)\n    \n    def refresh_gadget_icon(self, gadget):\n        \"\"\"Refresh icon for a specific gadget\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, '_gadget_buttons') and gadget.get_name() in self._gadget_buttons:\n            btn = self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()]\n            btn.setText(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n    \n    def _on_gadget_clicked(self):\n        \"\"\"Handle gadget button click\"\"\"\n        btn = self.sender()\n        if btn and hasattr(btn, '_gadget'):\n            try:\n                btn._gadget.on_click()\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in gadget click: {e}\")\n                import traceback\n                traceback.print_exc()\n    \n    def update_time(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the clock\"\"\"\n        current = datetime.now().strftime(\"%H:%M\")\n        self.time_label.setText(current)\n    \n    def switch_desktop(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Switch to specified desktop\"\"\"\n        self.desktop_manager.switch_to_desktop(desktop_id)\n        # Update window list to show new desktop's windows\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def on_desktop_changed(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Handle desktop change\"\"\"\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_desktop_buttons(self):\n        \"\"\"Update desktop button states\"\"\"\n        current = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n        for i, btn in enumerate(self.desktop_buttons):\n            btn.setChecked(i == current)\n            if i == current:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #666666; font-weight: bold;\")\n            else:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #4a4a4a;\")\n    \n    def build_menus_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Build the dynamic menus\"\"\"\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        \n        # Light theme for menu\n        menu.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QMenu {\n                background-color: #f5f5f5;\n                color: #333333;\n                border: 1px solid #cccccc;\n                padding: 5px;\n            }\n            QMenu::item {\n                padding: 8px 25px;\n                border-radius: 3px;\n                color: #333333;\n            }\n            QMenu::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: white;\n            }\n            QMenu::separator {\n                height: 1px;\n                background-color: #cccccc;\n                margin: 5px 0px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        # Load dynamic menus from directory structure\n        for menu_data in self.menu_manager.load_menus():\n            if menu_data.get(\"type\") == \"branch\":\n                # Create top-level submenu\n                submenu = menu.addMenu(menu_data[\"name\"])\n                self.menu_manager._populate_menu(submenu, menu_data.get(\"items\", []))\n        \n        menu.addSeparator()\n        menu.addAction(\"Exit\", self.window().close)\n        \n        return menu\n    \n    def on_config_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle configuration dropdown selection\"\"\"\n        if index < 0:\n            return\n        \n        # Block signals temporarily to prevent re-triggering when resetting\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.config_combo.setCurrentIndex(-1)\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(False)\n        \n        # Open appropriate dialog\n        if index == 0:\n            self.open_gadget_config()\n        elif index == 1:\n            self.open_panel_config()\n        elif index == 2:\n            self.open_menu_config()\n        elif index == 3:\n            self.open_datetime_config()\n        elif index == 4:\n            self.open_about_dialog()\n    \n    def open_gadget_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open gadget configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.gadget_config_pyqt5 import GadgetConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = GadgetConfigDialog(self.gadget_manager, self)\n        if dialog.exec_():\n            self.load_gadgets()\n    \n    def open_panel_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open panel configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.panel_config_pyqt5 import PanelConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = PanelConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_menu_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open menu configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.menu_config_pyqt5 import MenuConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = MenuConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_datetime_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open date/time configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.datetime_config_pyqt5 import DateTimeConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = DateTimeConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_about_dialog(self):\n        \"\"\"Open About KosDWM dialog\"\"\"\n        from src.about_dialog_pyqt5 import AboutDialog\n        \n        dialog = AboutDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 215, "timestamp": 1784915202.0361176, "date": "2026-07-24", "name": "ReadFile"} -->

> Create a centralized HTTP API service in the panel:
> - Panel runs HTTP server on configurable port (default 8080)
> - Create PanelAPI class that manages endpoints
> - Gadgets register endpoints: `panel.api.register("/endpoint", handler_func)`
> - Support GET/POST methods
> - Handler functions receive request data and return response
> - Store registered endpoints in a dictionary: {path: (handler, methods)}
> - Threaded HTTP server to avoid blocking UI

<!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nPanel widget for KosDWM PyQt5 version\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom PyQt5.QtWidgets import (\n    QWidget, QHBoxLayout, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QPushButton, \n    QComboBox, QFrame, QMenu, QAction, QButtonGroup\n)\nfrom PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, QTimer\nfrom PyQt5.QtGui import QFont\n\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\nfrom src.panel_api_pyqt5 import PanelAPI\nfrom PyQt5.QtGui import QFont\n\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\n\nclass Panel(QFrame):\n    \"\"\"\n    Top panel with gadgets, menus, clock, and desktop switcher\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, parent=None):\n        super().__init__(parent)\n        # Height is controlled by parent window (30px)\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QFrame {\n                background-color: #333333;\n                border: none;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 8px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #555555;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: white;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        # Setup desktop manager\n        from src.desktop_manager_pyqt5 import DesktopManager\n        self.desktop_manager = DesktopManager(self)\n        self.desktop_manager.desktop_changed.connect(self.on_desktop_changed)\n        \n        # Setup menu manager\n        from src.menus_pyqt5 import MenuManager\n        self.menu_manager = MenuManager(self)\n        \n        # Setup window manager\n        from src.window_manager_pyqt5 import WindowManager\n        self.window_manager = WindowManager()\n        \n        self.setup_ui()\n        \n        # Timer for clock updates\n        self.timer = QTimer()\n        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_time)\n        self.timer.start(1000)\n        \n        # Timer for window list updates\n        self.window_timer = QTimer()\n        self.window_timer.timeout.connect(self.update_window_list)\n        self.window_timer.start(2000)  # Update every 2 seconds\n    \n    def setup_ui(self):\n        \"\"\"Setup the panel UI\"\"\"\n        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(5, 2, 5, 2)\n        layout.setSpacing(5)\n        \n        # Left side: Menus button with dynamic menu\n        self.menus_button = QPushButton(\"\u2630\")\n        self.menus_button.setFixedWidth(30)\n        self.menus_button.setMenu(self.build_menus_menu())\n        layout.addWidget(self.menus_button)\n        \n        # Window switcher dropdown\n        self.window_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.window_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\")\n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(self.window_manager.is_available())\n        self.window_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_window_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.window_combo)\n        \n        # Desktop buttons\n        self.desktop_group = QButtonGroup(self)\n        self.desktop_buttons = []\n        for i in range(4):\n            btn = QPushButton(str(i + 1))\n            btn.setFixedWidth(25)\n            btn.setFixedHeight(22)\n            btn.setCheckable(True)\n            btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked, d=i: self.switch_desktop(d))\n            self.desktop_group.addButton(btn)\n            self.desktop_buttons.append(btn)\n            layout.addWidget(btn)\n        \n        # Middle: Gadgets frame\n        self.gadgets_frame = QFrame()\n        self.gadgets_frame.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: transparent;\")\n        self.gadgets_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.gadgets_frame)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setSpacing(5)\n        layout.addWidget(self.gadgets_frame)\n        \n        layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # Clock\n        self.time_label = QLabel(\"00:00\")\n        self.time_label.setFont(QFont(\"Arial\", 10, QFont.Bold))\n        layout.addWidget(self.time_label)\n        \n        # Config dropdown\n        self.config_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.config_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\u2699 Config\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Manage Gadgets\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Panel Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Menu Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Date/Time Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"About KosDWM\")\n        self.config_combo.setFixedWidth(120)\n        self.config_combo.setFixedHeight(22)\n        self.config_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_config_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.config_combo)\n        \n        # Load gadgets\n        self.load_gadgets()\n        \n        # Set current desktop\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        \n        # Initial window list population\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_window_list(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the window switcher dropdown with current windows\"\"\"\n        if not self.window_manager.is_available():\n            self.window_combo.setEnabled(False)\n            return\n        \n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(True)\n        \n        # Get current selection before clearing\n        current_id = None\n        current_index = self.window_combo.currentIndex()\n        if current_index > 0:  # 0 is placeholder\n            current_data = self.window_combo.itemData(current_index)\n            if current_data:\n                current_id = current_data.get('id')\n        \n        # Block signals while updating\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.window_combo.clear()\n        \n        # Add placeholder with icon\n        self.window_combo.addItem(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\", None)\n        \n        # Get windows and add to dropdown\n        windows = self.window_manager.get_windows()\n        selected_index = 0\n        \n        for i, window in enumerate(windows):\n            # Truncate long titles\n            title = window['title']\n            display_title = title[:32] + \"...\" if len(title) > 35 else title\n            \n            # Add desktop number prefix if not on current desktop\n            display_text = display_title\n            desktop_prefix = \"\"\n            if window['desktop'] != -1:  # -1 means sticky/all desktops\n                current_desktop = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n                if window['desktop'] != current_desktop:\n                    desktop_prefix = f\"[{window['desktop']+1}] \"\n                    display_text = f\"{desktop_prefix}{display_title}\"\n            \n            index = self.window_combo.count()\n            self.window_combo.addItem(display_text, window)\n            \n            # Set tooltip with full info\n            tooltip = f\"{desktop_prefix}{window['name']}\\nDesktop: {window['desktop']+1 if window['desktop'] >= 0 else 'All'}\"\n            self.window_combo.setItemData(index, tooltip, Qt.ToolTipRole)\n            \n            # Restore selection if this window was selected\n            if current_id and window['id'] == current_id:\n                selected_index = index\n        \n        # Restore selection\n        self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(selected_index)\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(False)\n    \n    def on_window_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle window selection from dropdown\"\"\"\n        if index <= 0:  # Placeholder selected\n            return\n        \n        window = self.window_combo.itemData(index)\n        if window and 'id' in window:\n            # Visual feedback - briefly highlight the combo\n            self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QComboBox {\n                    background-color: #666666;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 5px;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                    min-width: 120px;\n                    max-width: 200px;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            \n            # Activate the window\n            success = self.window_manager.activate_window(window['id'])\n            \n            # Reset to placeholder after activation\n            self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            \n            # Reset style after a short delay\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer\n            QTimer.singleShot(200, self._reset_window_combo_style)\n    \n    def _reset_window_combo_style(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset window combo to normal style\"\"\"\n        self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    \n    def load_gadgets(self):\n        \"\"\"Load gadgets into the panel\"\"\"\n        from src.gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetManager\n        \n        # Clear existing gadgets\n        while self.gadgets_layout.count():\n            item = self.gadgets_layout.takeAt(0)\n            if item.widget():\n                item.widget().deleteLater()\n        \n        # Create gadget manager\n        self.gadget_manager = GadgetManager()\n        \n        # Store gadget->button mapping for refresh\n        self._gadget_buttons = {}\n        \n        # Add enabled gadgets\n        for gadget in self.gadget_manager.get_enabled_gadgets():\n            btn = QPushButton(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setFixedSize(40, 22)\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n            # Store gadget reference on button to avoid lambda capture issues\n            btn._gadget = gadget\n            btn.clicked.connect(self._on_gadget_clicked)\n            self.gadgets_layout.addWidget(btn)\n            # Store mapping\n            self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()] = btn\n            # Set panel reference on gadget for refresh notifications\n            gadget.set_panel(self)\n    \n    def refresh_gadget_icon(self, gadget):\n        \"\"\"Refresh icon for a specific gadget\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, '_gadget_buttons') and gadget.get_name() in self._gadget_buttons:\n            btn = self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()]\n            btn.setText(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n    \n    def _on_gadget_clicked(self):\n        \"\"\"Handle gadget button click\"\"\"\n        btn = self.sender()\n        if btn and hasattr(btn, '_gadget'):\n            try:\n                btn._gadget.on_click()\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in gadget click: {e}\")\n                import traceback\n                traceback.print_exc()\n    \n    def update_time(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the clock\"\"\"\n        current = datetime.now().strftime(\"%H:%M\")\n        self.time_label.setText(current)\n    \n    def switch_desktop(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Switch to specified desktop\"\"\"\n        self.desktop_manager.switch_to_desktop(desktop_id)\n        # Update window list to show new desktop's windows\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def on_desktop_changed(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Handle desktop change\"\"\"\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_desktop_buttons(self):\n        \"\"\"Update desktop button states\"\"\"\n        current = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n        for i, btn in enumerate(self.desktop_buttons):\n            btn.setChecked(i == current)\n            if i == current:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #666666; font-weight: bold;\")\n            else:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #4a4a4a;\")\n    \n    def build_menus_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Build the dynamic menus\"\"\"\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        \n        # Light theme for menu\n        menu.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QMenu {\n                background-color: #f5f5f5;\n                color: #333333;\n                border: 1px solid #cccccc;\n                padding: 5px;\n            }\n            QMenu::item {\n                padding: 8px 25px;\n                border-radius: 3px;\n                color: #333333;\n            }\n            QMenu::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: white;\n            }\n            QMenu::separator {\n                height: 1px;\n                background-color: #cccccc;\n                margin: 5px 0px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        # Load dynamic menus from directory structure\n        for menu_data in self.menu_manager.load_menus():\n            if menu_data.get(\"type\") == \"branch\":\n                # Create top-level submenu\n                submenu = menu.addMenu(menu_data[\"name\"])\n                self.menu_manager._populate_menu(submenu, menu_data.get(\"items\", []))\n        \n        menu.addSeparator()\n        menu.addAction(\"Exit\", self.window().close)\n        \n        return menu\n    \n    def on_config_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle configuration dropdown selection\"\"\"\n        if index < 0:\n            return\n        \n        # Block signals temporarily to prevent re-triggering when resetting\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.config_combo.setCurrentIndex(-1)\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(False)\n        \n        # Open appropriate dialog\n        if index == 0:\n            self.open_gadget_config()\n        elif index == 1:\n            self.open_panel_config()\n        elif index == 2:\n            self.open_menu_config()\n        elif index == 3:\n            self.open_datetime_config()\n        elif index == 4:\n            self.open_about_dialog()\n    \n    def open_gadget_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open gadget configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.gadget_config_pyqt5 import GadgetConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = GadgetConfigDialog(self.gadget_manager, self)\n        if dialog.exec_():\n            self.load_gadgets()\n    \n    def open_panel_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open panel configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.panel_config_pyqt5 import PanelConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = PanelConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_menu_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open menu configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.menu_config_pyqt5 import MenuConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = MenuConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_datetime_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open date/time configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.datetime_config_pyqt5 import DateTimeConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = DateTimeConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_about_dialog(self):\n        \"\"\"Open About KosDWM dialog\"\"\"\n        from src.about_dialog_pyqt5 import AboutDialog\n        \n        dialog = AboutDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 216, "timestamp": 1784915231.8077438, "date": "2026-07-24", "name": "ReadFile"} -->

Height is controlled by parent window (30px)\n        self.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QFrame {\n                background-color: #333333;\n                border: none;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 8px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #555555;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: white;\n                font-size: 11px;\n            }\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        # Setup desktop manager\n        from src.desktop_manager_pyqt5 import DesktopManager\n        self.desktop_manager = DesktopManager(self)\n        self.desktop_manager.desktop_changed.connect(self.on_desktop_changed)\n        \n        # Setup menu manager\n        from src.menus_pyqt5 import MenuManager\n        self.menu_manager = MenuManager(self)\n        \n        # Setup window manager\n        from src.window_manager_pyqt5 import WindowManager\n        self.window_manager = WindowManager()\n        \n        # Initialize Panel API server (before setup_ui so gadgets can access it)\n        self.api = PanelAPI(port=8080)\n        self.api.start()\n        \n        self.setup_ui()\n        \n        # Timer for clock updates\n        self.timer = QTimer()\n        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_time)\n        self.timer.start(1000)\n        \n        # Timer for window list updates\n        self.window_timer = QTimer()\n        self.window_timer.timeout.connect(self.update_window_list)\n        self.window_timer.start(2000)  # Update every 2 seconds\n    \n    def setup_ui(self):\n        \"\"\"Setup the panel UI\"\"\"\n        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(5, 2, 5, 2)\n        layout.setSpacing(5)\n        \n        # Left side: Menus button with dynamic menu\n        self.menus_button = QPushButton(\"\u2630\")\n        self.menus_button.setFixedWidth(30)\n        self.menus_button.setMenu(self.build_menus_menu())\n        layout.addWidget(self.menus_button)\n        \n        # Window switcher dropdown\n        self.window_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.window_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\")\n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(self.window_manager.is_available())\n        self.window_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_window_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.window_combo)\n        \n        # Desktop buttons\n        self.desktop_group = QButtonGroup(self)\n        self.desktop_buttons = []\n        for i in range(4):\n            btn = QPushButton(str(i + 1))\n            btn.setFixedWidth(25)\n            btn.setFixedHeight(22)\n            btn.setCheckable(True)\n            btn.clicked.connect(lambda checked, d=i: self.switch_desktop(d))\n            self.desktop_group.addButton(btn)\n            self.desktop_buttons.append(btn)\n            layout.addWidget(btn)\n        \n        # Middle: Gadgets frame\n        self.gadgets_frame = QFrame()\n        self.gadgets_frame.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: transparent;\")\n        self.gadgets_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.gadgets_frame)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)\n        self.gadgets_layout.setSpacing(5)\n        layout.addWidget(self.gadgets_frame)\n        \n        layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # Clock\n        self.time_label = QLabel(\"00:00\")\n        self.time_label.setFont(QFont(\"Arial\", 10, QFont.Bold))\n        layout.addWidget(self.time_label)\n        \n        # Config dropdown\n        self.config_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.config_combo.setPlaceholderText(\"\u2699 Config\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Manage Gadgets\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Panel Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Menu Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"Date/Time Settings\")\n        self.config_combo.addItem(\"About KosDWM\")\n        self.config_combo.setFixedWidth(120)\n        self.config_combo.setFixedHeight(22)\n        self.config_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_config_selected)\n        layout.addWidget(self.config_combo)\n        \n        # Load gadgets\n        self.load_gadgets()\n        \n        # Set current desktop\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        \n        # Initial window list population\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_window_list(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the window switcher dropdown with current windows\"\"\"\n        if not self.window_manager.is_available():\n            self.window_combo.setEnabled(False)\n            return\n        \n        self.window_combo.setEnabled(True)\n        \n        # Get current selection before clearing\n        current_id = None\n        current_index = self.window_combo.currentIndex()\n        if current_index > 0:  # 0 is placeholder\n            current_data = self.window_combo.itemData(current_index)\n            if current_data:\n                current_id = current_data.get('id')\n        \n        # Block signals while updating\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.window_combo.clear()\n        \n        # Add placeholder with icon\n        self.window_combo.addItem(\"\ud83e\ude9f Windows\", None)\n        \n        # Get windows and add to dropdown\n        windows = self.window_manager.get_windows()\n        selected_index = 0\n        \n        for i, window in enumerate(windows):\n            # Truncate long titles\n            title = window['title']\n            display_title = title[:32] + \"...\" if len(title) > 35 else title\n            \n            # Add desktop number prefix if not on current desktop\n            display_text = display_title\n            desktop_prefix = \"\"\n            if window['desktop'] != -1:  # -1 means sticky/all desktops\n                current_desktop = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n                if window['desktop'] != current_desktop:\n                    desktop_prefix = f\"[{window['desktop']+1}] \"\n                    display_text = f\"{desktop_prefix}{display_title}\"\n            \n            index = self.window_combo.count()\n            self.window_combo.addItem(display_text, window)\n            \n            # Set tooltip with full info\n            tooltip = f\"{desktop_prefix}{window['name']}\\nDesktop: {window['desktop']+1 if window['desktop'] >= 0 else 'All'}\"\n            self.window_combo.setItemData(index, tooltip, Qt.ToolTipRole)\n            \n            # Restore selection if this window was selected\n            if current_id and window['id'] == current_id:\n                selected_index = index\n        \n        # Restore selection\n        self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(selected_index)\n        self.window_combo.blockSignals(False)\n    \n    def on_window_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle window selection from dropdown\"\"\"\n        if index <= 0:  # Placeholder selected\n            return\n        \n        window = self.window_combo.itemData(index)\n        if window and 'id' in window:\n            # Visual feedback - briefly highlight the combo\n            self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n                QComboBox {\n                    background-color: #666666;\n                    color: white;\n                    border: none;\n                    padding: 2px 5px;\n                    font-size: 11px;\n                    min-width: 120px;\n                    max-width: 200px;\n                }\n            \"\"\")\n            \n            # Activate the window\n            success = self.window_manager.activate_window(window['id'])\n            \n            # Reset to placeholder after activation\n            self.window_combo.setCurrentIndex(0)\n            \n            # Reset style after a short delay\n            from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer\n            QTimer.singleShot(200, self._reset_window_combo_style)\n    \n    def _reset_window_combo_style(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset window combo to normal style\"\"\"\n        self.window_combo.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QComboBox {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: none;\n                padding: 2px 5px;\n                font-size: 11px;\n                min-width: 120px;\n                max-width: 200px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::drop-down {\n                border: none;\n                width: 20px;\n            }\n            QComboBox::down-arrow {\n                image: none;\n                border-left: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-right: 4px solid transparent;\n                border-top: 6px solid white;\n                margin-right: 5px;\n            }\n            QComboBox QAbstractItemView {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: white;\n                border: 1px solid #666666;\n                selection-background-color: #666666;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    \n    def load_gadgets(self):\n        \"\"\"Load gadgets into the panel\"\"\"\n        from src.gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetManager\n        \n        # Clear existing gadgets\n        while self.gadgets_layout.count():\n            item = self.gadgets_layout.takeAt(0)\n            if item.widget():\n                item.widget().deleteLater()\n        \n        # Create gadget manager\n        self.gadget_manager = GadgetManager()\n        \n        # Store gadget->button mapping for refresh\n        self._gadget_buttons = {}\n        \n        # Add enabled gadgets - pass panel reference so gadgets can access API\n        for gadget in self.gadget_manager.get_enabled_gadgets():\n            btn = QPushButton(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setFixedSize(40, 22)\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n            # Store gadget reference on button to avoid lambda capture issues\n            btn._gadget = gadget\n            btn.clicked.connect(self._on_gadget_clicked)\n            self.gadgets_layout.addWidget(btn)\n            # Store mapping\n            self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()] = btn\n            # Set panel reference on gadget for refresh notifications and API access\n            gadget.set_panel(self)\n    \n    def refresh_gadget_icon(self, gadget):\n        \"\"\"Refresh icon for a specific gadget\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, '_gadget_buttons') and gadget.get_name() in self._gadget_buttons:\n            btn = self._gadget_buttons[gadget.get_name()]\n            btn.setText(gadget.get_icon())\n            btn.setToolTip(gadget.get_tooltip())\n    \n    def _on_gadget_clicked(self):\n        \"\"\"Handle gadget button click\"\"\"\n        btn = self.sender()\n        if btn and hasattr(btn, '_gadget'):\n            try:\n                btn._gadget.on_click()\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error in gadget click: {e}\")\n                import traceback\n                traceback.print_exc()\n    \n    def update_time(self):\n        \"\"\"Update the clock\"\"\"\n        current = datetime.now().strftime(\"%H:%M\")\n        self.time_label.setText(current)\n    \n    def switch_desktop(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Switch to specified desktop\"\"\"\n        self.desktop_manager.switch_to_desktop(desktop_id)\n        # Update window list to show new desktop's windows\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def on_desktop_changed(self, desktop_id):\n        \"\"\"Handle desktop change\"\"\"\n        self.update_desktop_buttons()\n        self.update_window_list()\n    \n    def update_desktop_buttons(self):\n        \"\"\"Update desktop button states\"\"\"\n        current = self.desktop_manager.current_desktop\n        for i, btn in enumerate(self.desktop_buttons):\n            btn.setChecked(i == current)\n            if i == current:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #666666; font-weight: bold;\")\n            else:\n                btn.setStyleSheet(\"background-color: #4a4a4a;\")\n    \n    def build_menus_menu(self):\n        \"\"\"Build the dynamic menus\"\"\"\n        menu = QMenu(self)\n        \n        # Light theme for menu\n        menu.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QMenu {\n                background-color: #f5f5f5;\n                color: #333333;\n                border: 1px solid #cccccc;\n                padding: 5px;\n            }\n            QMenu::item {\n                padding: 8px 25px;\n                border-radius: 3px;\n                color: #333333;\n            }\n            QMenu::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: white;\n            }\n            QMenu::separator {\n                height: 1px;\n                background-color: #cccccc;\n                margin: 5px 0px;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        # Load dynamic menus from directory structure\n        for menu_data in self.menu_manager.load_menus():\n            if menu_data.get(\"type\") == \"branch\":\n                # Create top-level submenu\n                submenu = menu.addMenu(menu_data[\"name\"])\n                self.menu_manager._populate_menu(submenu, menu_data.get(\"items\", []))\n        \n        menu.addSeparator()\n        menu.addAction(\"Exit\", self.window().close)\n        \n        return menu\n    \n    def on_config_selected(self, index):\n        \"\"\"Handle configuration dropdown selection\"\"\"\n        if index < 0:\n            return\n        \n        # Block signals temporarily to prevent re-triggering when resetting\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(True)\n        self.config_combo.setCurrentIndex(-1)\n        self.config_combo.blockSignals(False)\n        \n        # Open appropriate dialog\n        if index == 0:\n            self.open_gadget_config()\n        elif index == 1:\n            self.open_panel_config()\n        elif index == 2:\n            self.open_menu_config()\n        elif index == 3:\n            self.open_datetime_config()\n        elif index == 4:\n            self.open_about_dialog()\n    \n    def open_gadget_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open gadget configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.gadget_config_pyqt5 import GadgetConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = GadgetConfigDialog(self.gadget_manager, self)\n        if dialog.exec_():\n            self.load_gadgets()\n    \n    def open_panel_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open panel configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.panel_config_pyqt5 import PanelConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = PanelConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_menu_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open menu configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.menu_config_pyqt5 import MenuConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = MenuConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_datetime_config(self):\n        \"\"\"Open date/time configuration\"\"\"\n        from src.datetime_config_pyqt5 import DateTimeConfigDialog\n        \n        dialog = DateTimeConfigDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def open_about_dialog(self):\n        \"\"\"Open About KosDWM dialog\"\"\"\n        from src.about_dialog_pyqt5 import AboutDialog\n        \n        dialog = AboutDialog(self)\n        dialog.exec_()\n    \n    def closeEvent(self, event):\n        \"\"\"Clean up on close - stop the API server\"\"\"\n        if hasattr(self, 'api') and self.api:\n            self.api.stop()\n        event.accept()\n</contentOfFile>\n</WriteFile>", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 216, "timestamp": 1784915255.9575598, "date": "2026-07-24", "thinking": "", "prompt_tokens": 52186, "response_tokens": 3436} -->

> """
> Panel API Service for KosDWM PyQt5
> 
> Provides a centralized HTTP API server that allows gadgets to register
> custom endpoints for external communication.
> """

> import threading

>
