external communication.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport threading\nfrom http.server import HTTPServer, BaseHTTPRequestHandler\nfrom urllib.parse import urlparse, parse_qs\n\n\nclass PanelAPI:\n    \"\"\"\n    Centralized HTTP API service for the KosDWM panel.\n    \n    Gadgets can register endpoints using:\n        panel.api.register(\"/endpoint\", handler_func, methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\"])\n    \n    The server runs in a separate thread to avoid blocking the UI.\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, port=8080):\n        self.port = port\n        self.endpoints = {}  # {path: (handler_func, methods)}\n        self.server = None\n        self.server_thread = None\n        self._running = False\n        \n        # Register default endpoints\n        self._register_defaults()\n    \n    def _register_defaults(self):\n        \"\"\"Register default API endpoints\"\"\"\n        self.register(\"/api/status\", self._handle_status, methods=[\"GET\"])\n        self.register(\"/api/endpoints\", self._handle_endpoints_list, methods=[\"GET\"])\n    \n    def register(self, path, handler, methods=None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Register an endpoint with a handler function.\n        \n        Args:\n            path: URL path (e.g., \"/api/myendpoint\")\n            handler: Callable that receives request data and returns response\n            methods: List of HTTP methods allowed (default: [\"GET\", \"POST\"])\n        \n        Returns:\n            True if registration successful, False otherwise\n        \"\"\"\n        if methods is None:\n            methods = [\"GET\", \"POST\"]\n        \n        # Normalize path\n        path = path if path.startswith(\"/\") else \"/\" + path\n        \n        # Validate handler is callable\n        if not callable(handler):\n            print(f\"[PanelAPI] Error: Handler for {path} is not callable\")\n            return False\n        \n        # Store endpoint\n        self.endpoints[path] = {\n            'handler': handler,\n            'methods': [m.upper() for m in methods]\n        }\n        \n        print(f\"[PanelAPI] Registered endpoint: {path} [{', '.join(methods)}]\")\n        return True\n    \n    def unregister(self, path):\n        \"\"\"\n        Unregister an endpoint.\n        \n        Args:\n            path: URL path to unregister\n        \n        Returns:\n            True if unregistered, False if not found\n        \"\"\"\n        path = path if path.startswith(\"/\") else \"/\" + path\n        \n        if path in self.endpoints:\n            del self.endpoints[path]\n            print(f\"[PanelAPI] Unregistered endpoint: {path}\")\n            return True\n        return False\n    \n    def start(self):\n        \"\"\"Start the HTTP server in a separate thread\"\"\"\n        if self._running:\n            print(f\"[PanelAPI] Server already running on port {self.port}\")\n            return\n        \n        try:\n            self.server = ThreadedHTTPServer(('localhost', self.port), self._create_handler())\n            self.server.panel_api = self  # Give handler access to API\n            \n            self.server_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._serve, daemon=True)\n            self.server_thread.start()\n            self._running = True\n            \n            print(f\"[PanelAPI] HTTP server started on http://localhost:{self.port}\")\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[PanelAPI] Failed to start server: {e}\")\n    \n    def _serve(self):\n        \"\"\"Server thread target\"\"\"\n        try:\n            self.server.serve_forever()\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"[PanelAPI] Server error: {e}\")\n    \n    def stop(self):\n        \"\"\"Stop the HTTP server\"\"\"\n        if not self._running or not self.server:\n            return\n        \n        self.server.shutdown()\n        self.server.server_close()\n        self._running = False\n        print(f\"[PanelAPI] HTTP server stopped\")\n    \n    def is_running(self):\n        \"\"\"Check if server is running\"\"\"\n        return self._running\n    \n    def get_endpoints(self):\n        \"\"\"Get list of registered endpoints\"\"\"\n        return {\n            path: {'methods': info['methods']}\n            for path, info in self.endpoints.items()\n        }\n    \n    def _handle_status(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"Default status endpoint handler\"\"\"\n        return {\n            'status': 'ok',\n            'service': 'KosDWM Panel API',\n            'endpoints_registered': len(self.endpoints)\n        }\n    \n    def _handle_endpoints_list(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"List all registered endpoints\"\"\"\n        return {\n            'endpoints': self.get_endpoints()\n        }\n    \n    def _create_handler(self):\n        \"\"\"Factory to create request handler class with access to PanelAPI\"\"\"\n        panel_api = self\n        \n        class PanelAPIHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\n            def log_message(self, format, *args):\n                \"\"\"Suppress default logging\"\"\"\n                pass\n            \n            def do_GET(self):\n                self._handle_request(\"GET\")\n            \n            def do_POST(self):\n                self._handle_request(\"POST\")\n            \n            def do_OPTIONS(self):\n                \"\"\"Handle CORS preflight requests\"\"\"\n                self.send_response(200)\n                self.send_header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*')\n                self.send_header('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, OPTIONS')\n                self.send_header('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Content-Type')\n                self.end_headers()\n            \n            def _handle_request(self, method):\n                parsed = urlparse(self.path)\n                path = parsed.path\n                \n                # Find matching endpoint\n                endpoint = panel_api.endpoints.get(path)\n                \n                if not endpoint:\n                    self._send_error(404, f\"Endpoint not found: {path}\")\n                    return\n                \n                # Check method is allowed\n                if method not in endpoint['methods']:\n                    self._send_error(405, f\"Method {method} not allowed for {path}\")\n                    return\n                \n                # Parse request data\n                request_data = {\n                    'method': method,\n                    'path': path,\n                    'query': parse_qs(parsed.query),\n                    'headers': dict(self.headers)\n                }\n                \n                # Read body for POST requests\n                if method == \"POST\":\n                    content_length = self.headers.get('Content-Length')\n                    if content_length:\n                        body = self.rfile.read(int(content_length))\n                        try:\n                            request_data['body'] = json.loads(body.decode('utf-8'))\n                        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                            request_data['body'] = body.decode('utf-8')\n                \n                # Call handler\n                try:\n                    response = endpoint['handler'](request_data)\n                    \n                    # Ensure response is dict or string\n                    if not isinstance(response, (dict, list, str)):\n                        response = {'result': str(response)}\n                    \n                    self._send_json(200, response)\n                    \n                except Exception as e:\n                    print(f\"[PanelAPI] Handler error for {path}: {e}\")\n                    self._send_error(500, f\"Handler error: {str(e)}\")\n            \n            def _send_json(self, status_code, data):\n                \"\"\"Send JSON response\"\"\"\n                self.send_response(status_code)\n                self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/json')\n                self.send_header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*')\n                self.end_headers()\n                \n                if isinstance(data, str):\n                    self.wfile.write(data.encode('utf-8'))\n                else:\n                    self.wfile.write(json.dumps(data).encode('utf-8'))\n            \n            def _send_error(self, status_code, message):\n                \"\"\"Send error response\"\"\"\n                self.send_response(status_code)\n                self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/json')\n                self.send_header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*')\n                self.end_headers()\n                \n                error_response = {\n                    'error': message,\n                    'status': status_code\n                }\n                self.wfile.write(json.dumps(error_response).encode('utf-8'))\n        \n        return PanelAPIHandler\n\n\nclass ThreadedHTTPServer(HTTPServer):\n    \"\"\"Threaded HTTP server to handle multiple concurrent requests\"\"\"\n    allow_reuse_address = True\n\n\n# Example usage and testing\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import time\n    \n    # Create API instance\n    api = PanelAPI(port=8080)\n    \n    # Register example endpoints\n    def hello_handler(request):\n        return {'message': 'Hello from KosDWM!', 'method': request['method']}\n    \n    def echo_handler(request):\n        return {\n            'echo': request.get('body', {}),\n            'query': request.get('query', {})\n        }\n    \n    api.register(\"/api/hello\", hello_handler, methods=[\"GET\"])\n    api.register(\"/api/echo\", echo_handler, methods=[\"POST\"])\n    \n    # Start server\n    api.start()\n    \n    print(\"\\nTest with:\")\n    print(\"  curl http://localhost:8080/api/status\")\n    print(\"  curl http://localhost:8080/api/hello\")\n    print(\"  curl -X POST -d '{\\\"test\\\":\\\"data\\\"}' http://localhost:8080/api/echo\")\n    print(\"\\nPress Ctrl+C to stop...\")\n    \n    try:\n        while True:\n            time.sleep(1)\n    except KeyboardInterrupt:\n        api.stop()\n        print(\"\\nServer stopped\")\n", toolbar.addWidget(QLabel(\"Font:\"))\n        self.font_combo = QFontComboBox()\n        self.font_combo.currentFontChanged.connect(self._change_font)\n        toolbar.addWidget(self.font_combo)\n self.priority_combo = QComboBox()\n        self.priority_combo.addItems([\"low\", \"medium\", \"high\"])\n        self.priority_combo.setCurrentText(self.initial_priority)\n        }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #5a5a5a;\n            }\n            QPushButton:checked {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    \n    def _toggle_bold(self):\n        if self.bold_btn.isChecked():\n            self.editor.setFontWeight(QFont.Bold)\n        else:\n            self.editor.setFontWeight(QFont.Normal)\n    \n    def _toggle_italic(self):\n        self.editor.setFontItalic(self.italic_btn.isChecked())\n    \n    def _toggle_underline(self):\n        self.editor.setFontUnderline(self.underline_btn.isChecked())\n    \n    def _change_font_size(self, size):\n        self.editor.setFontPointSize(size)\n    \n    def _change_font(self, font):\n        self.editor.setFontFamily(font.family())\n    \n    def _change_color(self):\n        color = QColorDialog.getColor(self.editor.textColor(), self)\n        if color.isValid():\n            self.editor.setTextColor(color)\n    \n    def _update_toolbar(self):\n        \"\"\"Update toolbar buttons based on current cursor format\"\"\"\n        fmt = self.editor.currentCharFormat()\n        self.bold_btn.setChecked(fmt.fontWeight() == QFont.Bold)\n        self.italic_btn.setChecked(fmt.fontItalic())\n        self.underline_btn.setChecked(fmt.fontUnderline())\n        self.size_spin.setValue(int(fmt.fontPointSize()) if fmt.fontPointSize() > 0 else 12)\n    \n    def get_data(self):\n        \"\"\"Return the notice data\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"title\": self.title_edit.text(),\n            \"content\": self.editor.toHtml(),\n            \"priority\": self.priority_combo.currentText(),\n            \"due_date\": self.due_edit.dateTime().toPyDateTime()\n        }\n\n\nclass NoticesStore:\n    \"\"\"Manages notices storage with thread-safe access\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self.notices = []\n        self.file_path = Path.home() / \".config\" / \"KosDWM\" / \"notices.json\"\n        self._lock = threading.RLock()  # Thread-safe lock\n        self.load()\n    \n    def load(self):\n        \"\"\"Load notices from file\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            if self.file_path.exists():\n                try:\n                    with open(self.file_path) as f:\n                        data = json.load(f)\n                        if isinstance(data, list):\n                            self.notices = [Notice.from_dict(n) for n in data]\n                except Exception as e:\n                    print(f\"Error loading notices: {e}\")\n    \n    def save(self):\n        \"\"\"Save notices to file\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            try:\n                self.file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n                with open(self.file_path, 'w') as f:\n                    json.dump([n.to_dict() for n in self.notices], f, indent=2)\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error saving notices: {e}\")\n    \n    def add(self, notice):\n        \"\"\"Add a new notice (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            self.notices.append(notice)\n            self.save()\n            return notice.id\n    \n    def remove(self, notice_id):\n        \"\"\"Remove a notice by ID (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            original_len = len(self.notices)\n            self.notices = [n for n in self.notices if n.id != notice_id]\n            removed = len(self.notices) < original_len\n            if removed:\n                self.save()\n            return removed\n    \n    def get(self, notice_id):\n        \"\"\"Get a notice by ID (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            for n in self.notices:\n                if n.id == notice_id:\n                    return n\n            return None\n    \n    def get_all(self):\n        \"\"\"Get all notices (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return list(self.notices)\n    \n    def get_active(self):\n        \"\"\"Get active (not completed) notices (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return [n for n in self.notices if not n.completed]\n    \n    def get_overdue(self):\n        \"\"\"Get overdue notices (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            now = datetime.now()\n            return [n for n in self.notices if n.due_date and n.due_date < now and not n.completed]\n\n\nclass NoticesGadget(GadgetBase):\n    \"\"\"\n    Notices gadget for managing reminders with HTTP API endpoints\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.store = NoticesStore()\n        self.active_count = len(self.store.get_active())\n        self.list_widget = None\n        \n        # Timer for periodic updates\n        self.timer = QTimer()\n        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_badge)\n        self.timer.start(60000)  # Update every minute\n    \n    def set_panel(self, panel):\n        \"\"\"Store panel reference and register HTTP API endpoints\"\"\"\n        super().set_panel(panel)\n        \n        # Register HTTP endpoints when panel is set\n        if self.api:\n            self._register_api_endpoints()\n    \n    def _register_api_endpoints(self):\n        \"\"\"Register HTTP API endpoints for notices\"\"\"\n        # GET /api/notices - list all notices\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/notices\", self._api_list_notices, methods=[\"GET\"])\n        \n        # POST /api/notices/add - add new notice\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/notices/add\", self._api_add_notice, methods=[\"POST\"])\n        \n        # POST /api/notices/delete - delete notice\n  self.initial_title = title\n        self.initial_content = content\n        self.initial_priority = priority\n        self.initial_due_date = due_date\n        \n        self._setup_ui()\n #4a90d9;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n    \n    def _toggle_bold(self):\n        if self.bold_btn.isChecked():\n            self.editor.setFontWeight(QFont.Bold)\n        else:\n            self.editor.setFontWeight(QFont.Normal)\n    \n    def _toggle_italic(self):\n        self.editor.setFontItalic(self.italic_btn.isChecked())\n    \n    def _toggle_underline(self):\n        self.editor.setFontUnderline(self.underline_btn.isChecked())\n    \n    def _change_font_size(self, size):\n        self.editor.setFontPointSize(size)\n    \n    def _change_font(self, font):\n        self.editor.setFontFamily(font.family())\n    \n    def _change_color(self):\n        color = QColorDialog.getColor(self.editor.textColor(), self)\n        if color.isValid():\n            self.editor.setTextColor(color)\n    \n    def _update_toolbar(self):\n        \"\"\"Update toolbar buttons based on current cursor format\"\"\"\n        fmt = self.editor.currentCharFormat()\n        self.bold_btn.setChecked(fmt.fontWeight() == QFont.Bold)\n        self.italic_btn.setChecked(fmt.fontItalic())\n        self.underline_btn.setChecked(fmt.fontUnderline())\n        self.size_spin.setValue(int(fmt.fontPointSize()) if fmt.fontPointSize() > 0 else 12)\n    \n    def get_data(self):\n        \"\"\"Return the notice data\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"title\": self.title_edit.text(),\n            \"content\": self.editor.toHtml(),\n            \"priority\": self.priority_combo.currentText(),\n            \"due_date\": self.due_edit.dateTime().toPyDateTime()\n        }\n\n\nclass NoticesStore:\n    \"\"\"Manages notices storage with thread-safe access\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self.notices = []\n        self.file_path = Path.home() / \".config\" / \"KosDWM\" / \"notices.json\"\n        self._lock = threading.RLock()  # Thread-safe lock\n        self.load()\n    \n    def load(self):\n        \"\"\"Load notices from file\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            if self.file_path.exists():\n                try:\n                    with open(self.file_path) as f:\n                        data = json.load(f)\n                        if isinstance(data, list):\n                            self.notices = [Notice.from_dict(n) for n in data]\n                except Exception as e:\n                    print(f\"Error loading notices: {e}\")\n    \n    def save(self):\n        \"\"\"Save notices to file\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            try:\n                self.file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n                with open(self.file_path, 'w') as f:\n                    json.dump([n.to_dict() for n in self.notices], f, indent=2)\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"Error saving notices: {e}\")\n    \n    def add(self, notice):\n        \"\"\"Add a new notice (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            self.notices.append(notice)\n            self.save()\n            return notice.id\n    \n    def remove(self, notice_id):\n        \"\"\"Remove a notice by ID (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            original_len = len(self.notices)\n            self.notices = [n for n in self.notices if n.id != notice_id]\n            removed = len(self.notices) < original_len\n            if removed:\n                self.save()\n            return removed\n    \n    def get(self, notice_id):\n        \"\"\"Get a notice by ID (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            for n in self.notices:\n                if n.id == notice_id:\n                    return n\n            return None\n    \n    def get_all(self):\n        \"\"\"Get all notices (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return list(self.notices)\n    \n    def get_active(self):\n        \"\"\"Get active (not completed) notices (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return [n for n in self.notices if not n.completed]\n    \n    def get_overdue(self):\n        \"\"\"Get overdue notices (thread-safe)\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            now = datetime.now()\n            return [n for n in self.notices if n.due_date and n.due_date < now and not n.completed]\n\n\nclass NoticesGadget(GadgetBase):\n    \"\"\"\n    Notices gadget for managing reminders with HTTP API endpoints\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.store = NoticesStore()\n        self.active_count = len(self.store.get_active())\n        self.list_widget = None\n        \n        # Timer for periodic updates\n        self.timer = QTimer()\n        self.timer.timeout.connect(self.update_badge)\n        self.timer.start(60000)  # Update every minute\n    \n    def set_panel(self, panel):\n        \"\"\"Store panel reference and register HTTP API endpoints\"\"\"\n        super().set_panel(panel)\n        \n        # Register HTTP endpoints when panel is set\n        if self.api:\n            self._register_api_endpoints()\n    \n    def _register_api_endpoints(self):\n        \"\"\"Register HTTP API endpoints for notices (RESTful pattern)\"\"\"\n        # GET /api/notices - list all notices\n        # POST /api/notices - create new notice\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/notices\", self._api_handle_notices, methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\"])\n        \n        # POST /api/notices/delete - delete notice (using POST for simplicity)\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/notices/delete\", self._api_delete_notice, methods=[\"POST\"])\n        \n        print(\"[NoticesGadget] HTTP API endpoints registered\")\n    \n    def _api_handle_notices(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"API handler: GET /api/notices (list) or POST /api/notices (create)\"\"\"\n        method = request_data.get('method', 'GET')\n        \n        if method == 'GET':\n            return self._api_list_notices(request_data)\n        elif method == 'POST':\n            return self._api_create_notice(request_data)\n        else:\n            return {\"error\": f\"Method {method} not supported\", \"status\": \"error\"}\n    \n    def _api_list_notices(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"API handler: GET /api/notices - list all notices\"\"\"\n        notices = self.store.get_all()\n        return {\n            \"notices\": [n.to_dict() for n in notices],\n            \"count\": len(notices),\n            \"active\": len(self.store.get_active()),\n            \"overdue\": len(self.store.get_overdue())\n        }\n    \n    def _api_create_notice(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"API handler: POST /api/notices - create new notice\"\"\"\n        body = request_data.get('body', {})\n        \n        # Validate required fields\n        title = body.get('title', '').strip()\n        if not title:\n            return {\"error\": \"Title is required\", \"status\": \"error\"}\n        \n        # Create notice from request data\n        content = body.get('content', '')\n        priority = body.get('priority', 'medium')\n        \n        # Parse due_date if provided\n        due_date = None\n        if body.get('due_date'):\n            try:\n                due_date = datetime.fromisoformat(body['due_date'])\n            except ValueError:\n                return {\"error\": \"Invalid due_date format. Use ISO format\", \"status\": \"error\"}\n        \n        # Create and add notice\n        notice = Notice(title=title, content=content, due_date=due_date, priority=priority)\n        notice_id = self.store.add(notice)\n        \n        # Update badge\n        self.update_badge()\n        \n        return {\n            \"status\": \"success\",\n            \"notice\": notice.to_dict(),\n            \"message\": f\"Notice '{title}' created successfully\"\n        }\n    \n    def _api_delete_notice(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"API handler: POST /api/notices/delete - delete notice by ID\"\"\"\n        body = request_data.get('body', {})\n        \n        # Get notice ID from body\n        notice_id = body.get('id')\n        if not notice_id:\n            # Try query params\n            query = request_data.get('query', {})\n            notice_id = query.get('id', [None])[0]\n        \n        if not notice_id:\n            return {\"error\": \"Notice ID is required\", \"status\": \"error\"}\n        \n        # Try to remove\n        removed = self.store.remove(notice_id)\n        \n        if removed:\n            self.update_badge()\n            return {\n                \"status\": \"success\",\n                \"message\": f\"Notice {notice_id} deleted\"\n            }\n        else:\n            return {\n                \"status\": \"error\",\n                \"error\": f\"Notice {notice_id} not found\"\n            }\n    \n    def get_name(self):\n        return \"notices\"\n    \n    def get_icon(self):\n        \"\"\"Return icon with badge count\"\"\"\n        active = len(self.store.get_active())\n        if active > 0:\n            return f\"\ud83d\udcdd{active}\"\n        return \"\ud83d\udcdd\"\n    \n    def get_tooltip(self):\n        active = len(self.store.get_active())\n        overdue = len(self.store.get_overdue())\n        if overdue > 0:\n            return f\"Notices: {active} active, {overdue} overdue\"\n        return f\"Notices: {active} active\"\n    \n    def get_description(self):\n        return \"Manage notices and reminders with due dates and priorities.\"\n    \n    def on_click(self):\n        \"\"\"Open notices dialog\"\"\"\n        # Get parent window properly\n        parent = QApplication.activeWindow()\n        \n        # Create dialog with proper parent - store as instance variable to prevent garbage collection\n        self.notices_dialog = QDialog(parent)\n        dialog = self.notices_dialog\n        dialog.setWindowTitle(\"Notices\")\n        dialog.setGeometry(100, 100, 500, 400)\n        \n        # Dark theme stylesheet (consistent with other dialogs)\n        dialog.setStyleSheet(\"\"\"\n            QDialog {\n                background-color: #1a1a1a;\n            }\n            QLabel {\n                color: #ffffff;\n            }\n            QListWidget {\n                background-color: #2d2d2d;\n                color: #ffffff;\n                border: 1px solid #444444;\n            }\n            QListWidget::item:selected {\n                background-color: #4a90d9;\n                color: #ffffff;\n            }\n            QListWidget::item:hover {\n                background-color: #3d3d3d;\n            }\n            QPushButton {\n                background-color: #4a4a4a;\n                color: #ffffff;\n                border: 1px solid #555555;\n                padding: 8px 16px;\n                border-radius: 4px;\n                font-weight: bold;\n            }\n            QPushButton:hover {\n                background-color: #5a5a5a;\n            }\n        \"\"\")\n        \n        layout = QVBoxLayout(dialog)\n        layout.setSpacing(10)\n        layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)\n        \n        # Header\n        header = QLabel(\"Your Notices\")\n        header.setStyleSheet(\"font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; color: #ffffff;\")\n        layout.addWidget(header)\n        \n        # List of notices\n        self.list_widget = QListWidget()\n        self.list_widget.setMinimumHeight(200)\n        \n        # Connect click and double-click handlers\n        self.list_widget.itemClicked.connect(self._on_notice_clicked)\n        self.list_widget.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self._on_notice_double_clicked)\n        \n        layout.addWidget(self.list_widget)\n        \n        # Buttons row\n        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()\n        \n        # Add button\n        add_btn = QPushButton(\"Add Notice\")\n        add_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self._add_notice())\n        btn_layout.addWidget(add_btn)\n        \n        # Delete button\n        delete_btn = QPushButton(\"Delete\")\n        delete_btn.clicked.connect(self._delete_selected_notice)\n        btn_layout.addWidget(delete_btn)\n        \n        btn_layout.addStretch()\n        \n        # Close button\n        close_btn = QPushButton(\"Close\")\n        close_btn.clicked.connect(dialog.accept)\n        btn_layout.addWidget(close_btn)\n        \n        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)\n        \n        # Refresh the list AFTER all widgets are created\n        self._refresh_notices_list()\n        \n        # Show dialog non-modally to prevent freezing\n        dialog.show()\n        dialog.raise_()\n        dialog.activateWindow()\n    \n    def _refresh_notices_list(self):\n        \"\"\"Refresh the notices list widget\"\"\"\n        if self.list_widget is None:\n            return\n        \n        self.list_widget.clear()\n        \n        for notice in self.store.get_active():\n            # Strip HTML for list display\n            plain_content = strip_html(notice.content)\n            # Show title and preview of content\n            item_text = notice.title\n            if plain_content:\n                preview = plain_content[:40] + \"...\" if len(plain_content) > 40 else plain_content\n                item_text += f\" - {preview}\"\n            if notice.priority != \"medium\":\n                item_text += f\" [{notice.priority}]\"\n            \n            # Create item and set data properly\n            item = QListWidgetItem(item_text)\n            item.setData(Qt.UserRole, notice.id)\n            self.list_widget.addItem(item)\n    \n    def _on_notice_clicked(self, item):\n        \"\"\"Handle single click on notice - just select the item\"\"\"\n        # Selection happens automatically, no action needed\n        pass\n    \n    def _on_notice_double_clicked(self, item):\n        \"\"\"Handle double click on notice - open full edit dialog\"\"\"\n        notice_id = item.data(Qt.UserRole)\n        if not notice_id:\n            return\n        \n        notice = self._get_notice_by_id(notice_id)\n        if not notice:\n            return\n        \n        # Open edit dialog with rich text editor\n        self._edit_notice(notice)\n        self._refresh_notices_list()\n    \n    def _get_notice_by_id(self, notice_id):\n        \"\"\"Get notice by ID\"\"\"\n        return self.store.get(notice_id)\n    \n    def _add_notice(self):\n        \"\"\"Add a new notice using rich text editor\"\"\"\n        parent = self.notices_dialog if hasattr(self, 'notices_dialog') else None\n        editor = RichTextEditorDialog(parent)\n        if editor.exec_() == QDialog.Accepted:\n            data = editor.get_data()\n            notice = Notice(\n                title=data[\"title\"],\n                content=data[\"content\"],\n                due_date=data[\"due_date\"],\n                priority=data[\"priority\"]\n            )\n            self.store.add(notice)\n            self._refresh_notices_list()\n            self.update_badge()\n    \n    def _delete_selected_notice(self):\n        \"\"\"Delete the selected notice\"\"\"\n        if self.list_widget is None:\n            return\n        \n        current_row = self.list_widget.currentRow()\n        if current_row < 0:\n            return\n        \n        item = self.list_widget.item(current_row)\n        notice_id = item.data(Qt.UserRole)\n        \n        if notice_id:\n            reply = QMessageBox.question(\n                self.list_widget.window(),\n                \"Confirm Delete\",\n                \"Delete this notice?\",\n                QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No\n            )\n            if reply == QMessageBox.Yes:\n                self.store.remove(notice_id)\n                self._refresh_notices_list()\n                self.update_badge()\n    \n    def _edit_notice(self, notice):\n        \"\"\"Edit an existing notice with rich text editor\"\"\"\n        parent = self.notices_dialog if hasattr(self, 'notices_dialog') else None\n        \n        editor = RichTextEditorDialog(\n            parent,\n            title=notice.title,\n            content=notice.content,\n            priority=notice.priority,\n            due_date=notice.due_date\n        )\n        \n        if editor.exec_() == QDialog.Accepted:\n            data = <!-- {"role": "tool", "content": "# KosDWM Agent Guide\n\n## Key Commands\n- **TkInter Version**: `python run.py`\n- **PyQt5 Version**: `python main_pyqt5.py`\n- Config: Edit `~/.config/KosDWM/config.json`\n- Menus: Create folders in `~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/`\n\n## Important Files\n\n### PyQt5 Version (Current)\n- Entry point: `main_pyqt5.py`\n- Panel: `src/panel_pyqt5.py`\n- Menus: `src/menus_pyqt5.py`\n- Gadgets: `src/gadgets_pyqt5.py`\n- Window Manager: `src/window_manager_pyqt5.py`\n- Desktop Manager: `src/desktop_manager_pyqt5.py`\n\n### Legacy TkInter Version\n- Entry point: `run.py`\n- Config manager: `src/config.py`\n- Helper functions: `src/functions.py`\n\n## Configuration Locations\n- Main config: `~/.config/KosDWM/config.json`\n- Gadgets config: `~/.config/KosDWM/gadgets.json`\n- Menus directory: `~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/`\n- Notices data: `~/.config/KosDWM/notices.json`\n\n## PyQt5 Features\n- **Window Switcher**: Dropdown showing running windows (wmctrl)\n- **Auto-Generative Menus**: Dynamic menus from directory structure\n  - Folders = submenus\n  - `config.json` = leaf menus (content windows)\n  - `.py` files = runnable scripts with `run()` function\n  - `windowScript` = looping command output\n- **Gadget System**: Clickable icons in panel\n  - **Notices Gadget**: Rich text editor with formatting (bold, italic, underline, color, font, size)\n- **Dark Theme**: All dialogs use dark background with light text\n  - `windowScript` = looping command output\n- **Gadget System**: Clickable icons in panel\n- **Light Theme**: All dialogs use light background with blue buttons\n\n## External Dependencies\n- Requires `wmctrl` command for window management\n- Requires `PyQt5` Python package (`pip install PyQt5`)\n- For TkInter version: requires `screeninfo` and standard Tkinter\n\n## Menu Structure Example\n```\n~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 Home/\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 About/\n\u2502   \u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 config.json       # {\"title\": \"About\", \"windowContent\": \"about.html\"}\n\u2502   \u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 about.html        # Static content\n\u2502   \u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 ok.py             # Optional: def run(window): ...\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 Test/\n\u2502       \u251c\u2500\u2500 config.json       # {\"windowScript\": \"lsof -i\", \"loop\": 5}\n\u2502       \u2514\u2500\u2500 ok.py\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 Scripts/\n    \u2514\u2500\u2500 my_tool.py            # def run(parent): ...\n```\n\n## Recent Changes (PyQt5)\n- Complete migration from TkInter to PyQt5\n- Added window switcher dropdown using wmctrl\n- Added auto-generative menu system\n- Added light theme for all dialogs\n- Fixed menu freeze with Qt.QueuedConnection\n- Gadget system now uses QMessageBox with styling\n", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 218, "timestamp": 1784915509.9696722, "date": "2026-07-24", "name": "ReadFile"} -->

Create API server\n        api = PanelAPI(port=8084)\n        api.start()\n        time.sleep(0.3)\n        \n        # Create multiple test gadgets\n        class TestGadget1(GadgetBase):\n            def get_name(self): return \"test1\"\n            def get_icon(self): return \"\ud83d\udd39\"\n            def get_tooltip(self): return \"Test 1\"\n            def on_click(self): pass\n            def set_panel(self, panel):\n                super().set_panel(panel)\n                self.register_endpoint(\"/api/test1\", lambda r: {\"gadget\": \"test1\"})\n        \n        class TestGadget2(GadgetBase):\n            def get_name(self): return \"test2\"\n            def get_icon(self): return \"\ud83d\udd38\"\n            def get_tooltip(self): return \"Test 2\"\n            def on_click(self): pass\n            def set_panel(self, panel):\n                super().set_panel(panel)\n                self.register_endpoint(\"/api/test2\", lambda r: {\"gadget\": \"test2\"})\n        \n        class MockPanel:\n            def __init__(self):\n                self.api = api\n        \n        panel = MockPanel()\n        \n        # Register both gadgets\n        gadget1 = TestGadget1()\n        gadget1.set_panel(panel)\n        \n        gadget2 = TestGadget2()\n        gadget2.set_panel(panel)\n        \n        time.sleep(0.3)\n        \n        # Verify both endpoints work\n        from urllib.request import urlopen\n        \n        response = urlopen(\"http://localhost:8084/api/test1\")\n        data = json.loads(response.read().decode())\n        assert data.get('gadget') == 'test1', \"TestGadget1 endpoint failed\"\n        print(\"\u2713 Gadget 1 endpoint works\")\n        \n        response = urlopen(\"http://localhost:8084/api/test2\")\n        data = json.loads(response.read().decode())\n        assert data.get('gadget') == 'test2', \"TestGadget2 endpoint failed\"\n        print(\"\u2713 Gadget 2 endpoint works\")\n        \n        # Check all registered endpoints\n        endpoints = api.get_endpoints()\n        assert '/api/test1' in endpoints, \"Test1 endpoint not registered\"\n        assert '/api/test2' in endpoints, \"Test2 endpoint not registered\"\n        print(f\"\u2713 All endpoints registered: {list(endpoints.keys())}\")\n        \n        api.stop()\n        return True\n        \n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"\u2717 Error: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return False\n\n\ndef print_curl_examples():\n    \"\"\"Print curl commands for manual testing\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"CURL TEST COMMANDS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"\"\"\n# Start KosDWM first, then test:\n\n# 1. Check API status\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/status | python -m json.tool\n\n# 2. List all notices\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/notices | python -m json.tool\n\n# 3. Create a notice\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:8080/api/notices \\\\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\\\n  -d '{\"title\":\"Meeting\",\"content\":\"<b>Team meeting</b>\",\"priority\":\"high\",\"due_date\":\"2024-12-31T10:00:00\"}' \\\\\n  | python -m json.tool\n\n# 4. Delete a notice (replace ID)\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:8080/api/notices/delete \\\\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\\\n  -d '{\"id\":\"YOUR-NOTICE-ID-HERE\"}' \\\\\n  | python -m json.tool\n\n# 5. List all registered endpoints\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/endpoints | python -m json.tool\n\"\"\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Run all tests\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"KOSDWM COMPLETE API TEST SUITE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    \n    results = []\n    \n    results.append((\"About Dialog\", test_about_dialog()))\n    results.append((\"Panel API\", test_panel_api()))\n    results.append((\"Notices Gadget\", test_notices_gadget()))\n    results.append((\"Multiple Gadgets\", test_multiple_gadgets()))\n    \n    # Print summary\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"TEST SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    \n    for name, passed in results:\n        status = \"\u2713 PASS\" if passed else \"\u2717 FAIL\"\n        print(f\"{status}: {name}\")\n    \n    total = len(results)\n    passed = sum(1 for _, p in results if p)\n    print(f\"\\nTotal: {passed}/{total} tests passed\")\n    \n    print_curl_examples()\n    \n    return passed == total\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    success = main()\n    sys.exit(0 if success else 1)\n</contentOfFile>\n</WriteFile>", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 218, "timestamp": 1784915530.2418013, "date": "2026-07-24", "thinking": "", "prompt_tokens": 97334, "response_tokens": 2486} -->

<!-- {"role": "assistant", "content": "<WriteFile>\n<fileName>AGENTS.md</fileName>\n<contentOfFile># KosDWM Agent Guide\n\n## Key Commands\n- **TkInter Version**: `python run.py`\n- **PyQt5 Version**: `python main_pyqt5.py`\n- Config: Edit `~/.config/KosDWM/config.json`\n- Menus: Create folders in `~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/`\n\n## Important Files\n\n### PyQt5 Version (Current)\n- Entry point: `main_pyqt5.py`\n- Panel: `src/panel_pyqt5.py`\n- Menus: `src/menus_pyqt5.py`\n- Gadgets: `src/gadgets_pyqt5.py`\n- Window Manager: `src/window_manager_pyqt5.py`\n- Desktop Manager: `src/desktop_manager_pyqt5.py`\n- Panel API: `src/panel_api_pyqt5.py`\n- About Dialog: `src/about_dialog_pyqt5.py`\n- Notices Gadget: `src/notices_gadget_pyqt5.py`\n\n### Legacy TkInter Version\n- Entry point: `run.py`\n- Config manager: `src/config.py`\n- Helper functions: `src/functions.py`\n\n## Configuration Locations\n- Main config: `~/.config/KosDWM/config.json`\n- Gadgets config: `~/.config/KosDWM/gadgets.json`\n- Menus directory: `~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/`\n- Notices data: `~/.config/KosDWM/notices.json`\n\n## PyQt5 Features\n\n### Window Switcher\n- Dropdown showing running windows (wmctrl)\n- Shows desktop numbers for windows on other desktops\n- Visual feedback on window activation\n\n### Auto-Generative Menus\n- Dynamic menus from directory structure\n  - Folders = submenus\n  - `config.json` = leaf menus (content windows)\n  - `.py` files = runnable scripts with `run()` function\n  - `windowScript` = looping command output\n\n### Gadget System\n- Clickable icons in panel\n- **Notices Gadget**: Rich text editor with formatting (bold, italic, underline, color, font, size)\n- Gadgets can register HTTP API endpoints via `panel.api.register()`\n\n### Dark Theme\n- All dialogs use dark background (#1a1a1a) with light text\n- Consistent styling across all configuration dialogs\n\n### About Window\n- Accessible from Config dropdown (\u2699 \u2192 About KosDWM)\n- Shows version from AUTOVERSION.py\n- Credits: B.K., OpenCode contributors\n- System info: Python version, Qt version, Platform\n\n## HTTP Panel API\n\nThe panel runs an HTTP API server on port 8080 (configurable) that allows gadgets to register custom endpoints.\n\n### Default Endpoints\n- `GET /api/status` - API status and endpoint count\n- `GET /api/endpoints` - List all registered endpoints\n\n### Notices Gadget Endpoints\n- `GET /api/notices` - List all notices\n- `POST /api/notices` - Create new notice\n- `POST /api/notices/delete` - Delete notice by ID\n\n### Creating Gadget API Endpoints\n\nGadgets can register HTTP endpoints by overriding `set_panel()`:\n\n```python\nclass MyGadget(GadgetBase):\n    def set_panel(self, panel):\n        super().set_panel(panel)\n        # Register endpoints when panel is set\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/mygadget/status\", self._api_status)\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/mygadget/action\", self._api_action, methods=[\"POST\"])\n    \n    def _api_status(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"Handle GET requests\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"gadget\": self.get_name(),\n            \"status\": \"active\",\n            \"method\": request_data.get('method')\n        }\n    \n    def _api_action(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"Handle POST requests\"\"\"\n        body = request_data.get('body', {})\n        # Process body data...\n        return {\"result\": \"success\"}\n```\n\n### Request Data Structure\n\nHandler functions receive a dictionary with:\n- `method`: HTTP method (\"GET\", \"POST\", etc.)\n- `path`: Request path\n- `query`: Query parameters dict\n- `headers`: Request headers dict\n- `body`: JSON body (for POST requests)\n\n### Testing with curl\n\n```bash\n# Check API status\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/status | python -m json.tool\n\n# List all notices\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/notices | python -m json.tool\n\n# Create a notice\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:8080/api/notices \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"title\":\"Meeting\",\"content\":\"<b>Team meeting</b>\",\"priority\":\"high\"}' \\\n  | python -m json.tool\n\n# Delete a notice\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:8080/api/notices/delete \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"id\":\"NOTICE-UUID-HERE\"}' \\\n  | python -m json.tool\n```\n\n## Thread Safety\n\nThe HTTP API runs in a separate thread. All gadget data access should be thread-safe:\n\n- `NoticesStore` uses `threading.RLock()` for all operations\n- UI updates should use Qt's signal/slot mechanism\n- Gadgets should minimize blocking operations in API handlers\n\n## External Dependencies\n- Requires `wmctrl` command for window management\n- Requires `PyQt5` Python package (`pip install PyQt5`)\n- For TkInter version: requires `screeninfo` and standard Tkinter\n\n## Menu Structure Example\n```\n~/.config/KosDWM/Menus/\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 Home/\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 About/\n\u2502   \u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 config.json       # {\"title\": \"About\", \"windowContent\": \"about.html\"}\n\u2502   \u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 about.html        # Static content\n\u2502   \u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 ok.py             # Optional: def run(window): ...\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 Test/\n\u2502       \u251c\u2500\u2500 config.json       # {\"windowScript\": \"lsof -i\", \"loop\": 5}\n\u2502       \u2514\u2500\u2500 ok.py\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 Scripts/\n    \u2514\u2500\u2500 my_tool.py            # def run(parent): ...\n```\n\n## Recent Changes (PyQt5)\n- Complete migration from TkInter to PyQt5\n- Added window switcher dropdown using wmctrl\n- Added auto-generative menu system\n- Added dark theme for all dialogs\n- Fixed menu freeze with Qt.QueuedConnection\n- Gadget system now uses QMessageBox with styling\n- **NEW**: Added About dialog with version and credits\n- **NEW**: HTTP Panel API for gadget endpoints\n- **NEW**: NoticesGadget with HTTP API endpoints (GET/POST/DELETE)\n- **NEW**: Thread-safe data access with RLock\n</contentOfFile>\n</WriteFile>", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 219, "timestamp": 1784915566.4904556, "date": "2026-07-24", "thinking": "", "prompt_tokens": 102489, "response_tokens": 1442} -->

<!-- {"role": "assistant", "content": "<WriteFile>\n<fileName>GADGET_API.md</fileName>\n<contentOfFile># Gadget HTTP API Documentation\n\n## Overview\n\nKosDWM provides a centralized HTTP API server that allows gadgets to register custom endpoints. This enables external applications and scripts to interact with gadgets programmatically.\n\n## How It Works\n\n1. The Panel starts an HTTP server on port 8080 (default)\n2. Gadgets register endpoints via `self.register_endpoint()`\n3. External clients make HTTP requests to these endpoints\n4. Handler functions process requests and return JSON responses\n\n## Quick Start\n\n### Basic Gadget with API\n\n```python\nfrom gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetBase\n\nclass MyGadget(GadgetBase):\n    def __init__(self):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.counter = 0\n    \n    def set_panel(self, panel):\n        super().set_panel(panel)\n        # Register API endpoints\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/mygadget/stats\", self._api_stats)\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/mygadget/increment\", self._api_increment, methods=[\"POST\"])\n    \n    def _api_stats(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"GET /api/mygadget/stats\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"gadget\": \"mygadget\",\n            \"counter\": self.counter,\n            \"status\": \"active\"\n        }\n    \n    def _api_increment(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"POST /api/mygadget/increment\"\"\"\n        self.counter += 1\n        self.refresh_icon()  # Update UI\n        return {\n            \"status\": \"success\",\n            \"new_counter\": self.counter\n        }\n    \n    # Required GadgetBase methods...\n    def get_name(self): return \"mygadget\"\n    def get_icon(self): return f\"\ud83d\udd22{self.counter}\"\n    def get_tooltip(self): return f\"Counter: {self.counter}\"\n    def on_click(self): pass\n```\n\n## API Reference\n\n### GadgetBase Methods\n\n#### `register_endpoint(path, handler, methods=None)`\nRegister an HTTP endpoint for your gadget.\n\n**Parameters:**\n- `path` (str): URL path (e.g., \"/api/mygadget/action\")\n- `handler` (callable): Function to handle requests\n- `methods` (list): HTTP methods allowed (default: [\"GET\", \"POST\"])\n\n**Returns:** `True` if registered successfully, `False` otherwise\n\n**Example:**\n```python\nself.register_endpoint(\"/api/mygadget/data\", self._api_data, methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\"])\n```\n\n#### `api` (property)\nAccess the PanelAPI instance directly (advanced usage).\n\n```python\nif self.api:\n    self.api.register(\"/custom/path\", handler)\n```\n\n### Handler Function\n\nHandler functions receive a `request_data` dictionary:\n\n```python\n{\n    \"method\": \"POST\",           # HTTP method\n    \"path\": \"/api/mygadget/action\",  # Request path\n    \"query\": {\"key\": [\"value\"]}, # Query parameters (dict of lists)\n    \"headers\": {\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"},  # Request headers\n    \"body\": {\"title\": \"Test\"}   # JSON body (POST requests only)\n}\n```\n\n**Return Value:**\n- Return a dict for JSON response: `return {\"status\": \"ok\"}`\n- Return a string for plain text: `return \"Hello\"`\n- Exceptions are caught and return 500 error\n\n## Complete Example: Counter Gadget\n\n```python\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nCounter Gadget with HTTP API\n\"\"\"\n\nimport threading\nfrom gadgets_pyqt5 import GadgetBase\n\nclass CounterGadget(GadgetBase):\n    def __init__(self):\n        super().__init__()\n        self._counter = 0\n        self._lock = threading.RLock()  # Thread-safe counter\n    \n    def set_panel(self, panel):\n        super().set_panel(panel)\n        # Register endpoints\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/counter\", self._api_get, methods=[\"GET\"])\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/counter/increment\", self._api_increment, methods=[\"POST\"])\n        self.register_endpoint(\"/api/counter/reset\", self._api_reset, methods=[\"POST\"])\n    \n    def _api_get(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"GET /api/counter - Get current value\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return {\n                \"counter\": self._counter,\n                \"gadget\": \"counter\"\n            }\n    \n    def _api_increment(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"POST /api/counter/increment - Increment counter\"\"\"\n        body = request_data.get('body', {})\n        amount = body.get('amount', 1)\n        \n        with self._lock:\n            self._counter += amount\n        \n        self.refresh_icon()  # Update panel display\n        \n        return {\n            \"status\": \"success\",\n            \"counter\": self._counter,\n            \"incremented_by\": amount\n        }\n    \n    def _api_reset(self, request_data):\n        \"\"\"POST /api/counter/reset - Reset to zero\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            old_value = self._counter\n            self._counter = 0\n        \n        self.refresh_icon()\n        \n        return {\n            \"status\": \"success\",\n            \"message\": f\"Reset from {old_value} to 0\"\n        }\n    \n    # Required methods\n    def get_name(self): return \"counter\"\n    def get_icon(self): return f\"\ud83d\udd22{self._counter}\"\n    def get_tooltip(self): return f\"Counter: {self._counter}\"\n    def on_click(self):\n        # Optional: Show dialog or increment on click\n        pass\n```\n\n## Testing with curl\n\n```bash\n# Get counter value\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/counter | python -m json.tool\n\n# Increment by 1\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:8080/api/counter/increment \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"amount\": 1}' | python -m json.tool\n\n# Reset\ncurl -X POST http://localhost:8080/api/counter/reset\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n### 1. Thread Safety\nAlways use locks when accessing shared data:\n\n```python\ndef __init__(self):\n    self._lock = threading.RLock()\n\ndef _api_handler(self, request):\n    with self._lock:\n        # Access shared data\n        return {\"data\": self._data}\n```\n\n### 2. Error Handling\nReturn proper error responses:\n\n```python\ndef _api_create(self, request):\n    body = request.get('body', {})\n    \n    if not body.get('name'):\n        return {\"error\": \"Name is required\", \"status\": \"error\"}\n    \n    # Process...\n    return {\"status\": \"success\"}\n```\n\n### 3. UI Updates\nUse `refresh_icon()` to update the panel display:\n\n```python\ndef _api_update(self, request):\n    self._data = request.get('body', {})\n    self.refresh_icon()  # Updates gadget icon in panel\n    return {\"status\": \"updated\"}\n```\n\n### 4. Path Naming\nUse consistent paths: `/api/<gadgetname>/<action>`\n\n```python\nself.register_endpoint(\"/api/notices\", self._api_list)\nself.register_endpoint(\"/api/notices/add\", self._api_add)\nself.register_endpoint(\"/api/notices/delete\", self._api_delete)\n```\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n### Endpoint not working?\n- Check that `set_panel()` was called\n- Verify the path starts with \"/\"\n- Check console for \"[PanelAPI] Registered endpoint:\" message\n\n### Handler not called?\n- Verify HTTP method matches registered methods\n- Check request URL is exact (no trailing slashes)\n- Test with `curl -v` to see full response\n\n### Data not persisting?\n- Ensure thread-safe access with locks\n- Save to file if needed (not automatic)\n- Check for exceptions in handler\n\n## Advanced Topics\n\n### Custom HTTP Methods\n```python\nself.register_endpoint(\"/api/mygadget/data\", handler, methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"DELETE\"])\n```\n\n### Path Parameters\nThe API doesn't support path parameters like `/api/items/{id}`.\nUse query parameters instead: `/api/items?id=123`\n\n### CORS Support\nThe API includes CORS headers for browser access:\n```\nAccess-Control-Allow-Origin: *\nAccess-Control-Allow-Methods: GET, POST, OPTIONS\n```\n\n## See Also\n\n- `src/notices_gadget_pyqt5.py` - Full implementation example\n- `src/panel_api_pyqt5.py` - Panel API implementation\n- `src/gadgets_pyqt5.py` - GadgetBase class\n</contentOfFile>\n</WriteFile>", "sessionId": 335, "rowId": 219, "timestamp": 1784915580.0063622, "date": "2026-07-24", "thinking": "", "prompt_tokens": 103939, "response_tokens": 1792} -->
